36	
37	function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {
38	  try {
39	    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
40	    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
41	  } catch {
42	    return iso;
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	function toISO(d: Date): string {
47	  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
48	}
49	
50	/** Earliest date out of a list; nulls ignored. Returns null if all null. */
51	function earliestISO(dates: (Date | null)[]): string | null {
52	  const isos = dates.filter((d): d is Date => !!d).map(toISO).sort();
53	  return isos[0] ?? null;
54	}
55	
56	/**
57	 * verifying  — JIT fetch in flight; save disabled.
58	 * ok         — server agrees with what we opened the screen with.
59	 * absent     — server has no row for this barcode (treat as ok — local
60	 *              view is the only truth; the correction will create it).
61	 * conflict   — server's on_hand or nearest_expiry differs from local;
62	 *              show prompt before letting user save.
63	 * offline    — fetch failed because network down; save disabled.
64	 * error      — fetch failed otherwise; save disabled.
65	 */
66	type VerifyStatus = 'verifying' | 'ok' | 'absent' | 'conflict' | 'offline' | 'error';
67	
68	function isOfflineError(err: unknown): boolean {
69	  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
70	  return (
71	    msg.includes('Network request failed') ||
72	    msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||
73	    msg.includes('Unable to resolve host') ||
74	    msg === 'JIT timeout'
75	  );
76	}
77	
78	export function EditStockScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
79	  const t = useT();
80	  const { palette } = useTheme();
81	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
82	  const { barcode } = route.params;
83	
84	  const [row, setRow] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);
85	  const [originalQty, setOriginalQty] = useState<number>(0);
86	  const [originalExpiry, setOriginalExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);
87	  const [qty, setQty] = useState<number>(0);
88	  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);
89	  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState<boolean>(false);
90	
91	  // Per-pack expiry. Mirrors ReceivingScreen: off by default; appears only
92	  // when qty > 1 and not noExpiry. When on, expand into N rows so the
93	  // guard can set a different expiry on each pack.
94	  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);
95	  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);
96	  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
97	
98	  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);
99	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
100	
101	  // JIT verification — fetch the server's authoritative on_hand +
102	  // nearest_expiry the moment the screen opens, in parallel with the
103	  // local read. If the server differs, we surface a "Stock has changed"
104	  // prompt before letting the user save. If the fetch fails (timeout /
105	  // offline / 5xx), we block the save entirely — corrections must be
106	  // committed against verified state per the cache-freshness plan.
107	  const [verifyStatus, setVerifyStatus] = useState<VerifyStatus>('verifying');
108	  const [serverQty, setServerQty] = useState<number | null>(null);
109	  const [serverExpiry, setServerExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);
110	
111	  useEffect(() => {
112	    let cancelled = false;
113	    (async () => {
114	      const r = await findStock(barcode);
115	      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);
116	      if (cancelled) return;
117	      if (!r) return;
118	      setRow(r);
119	      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);
120	      setOriginalExpiry(exp);
121	      setQty(r.on_hand);
122	      if (exp) {
123	        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));
124	        setNoExpiry(false);
125	      } else {
126	        setNoExpiry(true);
127	      }
128	    })();
129	    return () => { cancelled = true; };
130	  }, [barcode]);
131	
132	  // Fire the JIT fetch independently of the local read so neither
133	  // blocks the other. The 3 s timeout matches CatalogPickerScreen.
134	  useEffect(() => {
135	    let cancelled = false;
136	    const controller = new AbortController();
137	    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);
138	    (async () => {
139	      try {
140	        const remote = await api.fetch.stockOne(barcode);
141	        if (cancelled) return;
142	        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);
143	        const remoteExpiry = remote.nearest_expiry ?? null;
144	        setServerQty(remoteQty);
145	        setServerExpiry(remoteExpiry);
146	        setVerifyStatus('ok'); // narrowed to 'conflict' below once we know local values
147	      } catch (err) {
148	        if (cancelled) return;
149	        // 404 = no stock row on server; treat as authoritative "absent"
150	        // — the correction will create it. Same UX as a clean ok.
151	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
152	          setVerifyStatus('absent');
153	          return;
154	        }
155	        // 401 is handled centrally; clear navigates to Login.
156	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;
157	        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');
158	      } finally {
159	        clearTimeout(t);
160	      }
161	    })();
162	    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };
163	  }, [barcode]);
164	
165	  // Once both local and server have settled, classify ok-vs-conflict.
166	  // Conflict iff server differs from what the user is about to edit.
167	  // We use originalQty/originalExpiry as the "what the user opened
168	  // with" reference — those don't change unless the user explicitly
169	  // accepts the new server values.
170	  useEffect(() => {
171	    if (verifyStatus !== 'ok' || row === null || serverQty === null) return;
172	    const qtyDiffers = Math.abs(serverQty - originalQty) > 1e-9;
173	    const expiryDiffers = (serverExpiry ?? null) !== (originalExpiry ?? null);
174	    if (qtyDiffers || expiryDiffers) setVerifyStatus('conflict');
175	  }, [verifyStatus, row, serverQty, serverExpiry, originalQty, originalExpiry]);
176	
177	  // Keep the per-pack list in sync with qty when toggle is on. Seed new
178	  // rows with the bulk expiry so the guard only edits the differing ones.
179	  useEffect(() => {
180	    if (!perPackExpiry) return;
181	    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {
182	      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));
183	      if (prev.length === n) return prev;
184	      const next = [...prev];
185	      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);
186	      while (next.length > n) next.pop();
187	      return next;
188	    });
189	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);
190	
191	  // Auto-disable per-pack when the toggle's preconditions go away
192	  // (qty drops to 1, or guard checks "no expiry").
193	  useEffect(() => {
194	    if (perPackExpiry && (qty <= 1 || noExpiry)) {
195	      setPerPackExpiry(false);
196	    }
197	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, noExpiry]);
198	
199	  /** Single new_expiry sent to server when per-pack is off. */
200	  const newSingleExpiry = useMemo(() => {
201	    if (noExpiry || perPackExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;
202	    return toISO(expiryDateObj);
203	  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry, perPackExpiry]);
204	
205	  /** Array of expiries sent when per-pack is on. */
206	  const newExpiriesArray = useMemo<string[] | null>(() => {
207	    if (noExpiry || !perPackExpiry) return null;
208	    return perPackExpiries.map((d) => (d ? toISO(d) : '')).filter((s) => s.length > 0);
209	  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries]);
210	
211	  /** Whatever ends up as the locally-tracked "nearest" expiry. */
212	  const localNearestExpiry = useMemo(() => {
213	    if (noExpiry) return null;
214	    if (perPackExpiry) return earliestISO(perPackExpiries);
215	    return expiryDateObj ? toISO(expiryDateObj) : null;
216	  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries, expiryDateObj]);
217	
218	  const changed = useMemo(() => {
219	    if (!row) return false;
220	    if (qty !== originalQty) return true;
221	    if (localNearestExpiry !== originalExpiry) return true;
222	    // Per-pack with same nearest as original still counts as a change if
223	    // any pack carries a date different from the bulk default.
224	    if (perPackExpiry) return true;
225	    return false;
226	  }, [row, qty, originalQty, localNearestExpiry, originalExpiry, perPackExpiry]);
227

229	    ? `${row.pack_size} ${row.unit}`

232	  /**

238	  const acceptServerView = () => {

256	  /**

294	    }
295	  };
296

502	              onPress={() => { setNoExpiry(!noExpiry); haptic.tap(); }}
503	              style={styles.noExpiryRow}
504	            >
505	              <View
506	                style={[
507	                  styles.checkbox,
508	                  {
509	                    backgroundColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',
510	                    borderColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,
511	                  },
512	                ]}
513	              >
514	                {noExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}
515	              </View>
516	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
517	                {t('noExpiry')}
518	              </Text>
519	            </Pressable>
520	          </View>
521	        </ScrollView>
522	
523	        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>
524	          {/* Save is gated by verification: cannot commit a correction
525	              while we're still verifying, in conflict (user must
526	              resolve via the banner), or while verification failed
527	              (offline / error — user retries by going back and re-
528	              opening once network returns). */}
529	          <Button
530	            label={changed ? t('saveChanges') : t('noChanges')}
531	            onPress={submit}
532	            loading={saving}
533	            disabled={
534	              !changed ||
535	              verifyStatus === 'verifying' ||
536	              verifyStatus === 'conflict' ||
537	              verifyStatus === 'offline' ||
538	              verifyStatus === 'error'
539	            }
540	            size="lg"
541	            fullWidth
542	            leadingIcon={<Check size={22} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
543	          />
544	          <Button
545	            label={t('cancel')}
546	            variant="text"
547	            onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
548	            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
549	          />
550	        </View>
551	      </View>
552	    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
553	  );
554	}
555

558	  content: { padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },

1	import { getDb, now } from './database';
2	
3	export type StockStatus = 'ok' | 'low' | 'out';
4	
5	export type StockRow = {
6	  barcode: string;
7	  name: string;
8	  category: string | null;
9	  unit: string | null;
10	  pack_size: number | null;
11	  on_hand: number;
12	  threshold: number;
13	  updated_at: number;
14	};
15	
16	export function statusFor(row: Pick<StockRow, 'on_hand' | 'threshold'>): StockStatus {
17	  if (row.on_hand <= 0) return 'out';
18	  if (row.on_hand <= row.threshold) return 'low';
19	  return 'ok';
20	}
21	
22	export async function listStock(): Promise<StockRow[]> {
23	  const db = await getDb();
24	  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE ASC');
25	}
26	
27	export async function listLowOrOut(): Promise<StockRow[]> {
28	  const db = await getDb();
29	  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>(
30	    `SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE on_hand <= threshold ORDER BY on_hand ASC, name COLLATE NOCASE ASC`
31	  );
32	}
33	
34	export async function findStock(barcode: string): Promise<StockRow | null> {
35	  const db = await getDb();
36	  return (
37	    (await db.getFirstAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?', [barcode])) ??
38	    null
39	  );
40	}
41	
42	export async function upsertStock(row: {
43	  barcode: string;
44	  name: string;
45	  category: string | null;
46	  unit: string | null;
47	  pack_size?: number | null;
48	  on_hand?: number;
49	  threshold?: number;
50	}): Promise<void> {
51	  const db = await getDb();
52	  // COALESCE on pack_size so a later receipt that doesn't know the pack
53	  // size doesn't blank out a value learned from the catalog/registration.
54	  await db.runAsync(
55	    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)
56	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
57	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
58	       name=excluded.name,
59	       category=excluded.category,
60	       unit=excluded.unit,
61	       pack_size=COALESCE(excluded.pack_size, stock_levels.pack_size),
62	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
63	    [
64	      row.barcode,
65	      row.name,
66	      row.category,
67	      row.unit,
68	      row.pack_size ?? null,
69	      row.on_hand ?? 0,
70	      row.threshold ?? 0,
71	      now(),
72	    ]
73	  );
74	}
75	
76	/**
77	 * Replace the local cached stock row with the server's values. Used by
78	 * screens after a successful remote fetch so on-hand and threshold
79	 * reflect the source of truth.
80	 */
81	export async function syncStockFromRemote(row: {
82	  barcode: string;
83	  name: string;
84	  category: string | null;
85	  unit: string | null;
86	  pack_size: number | null;
87	  on_hand: number;
88	  threshold: number;
89	}): Promise<void> {
90	  const db = await getDb();
91	  await db.runAsync(
92	    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)
93	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
94	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
95	       name=excluded.name,
96	       category=excluded.category,
97	       unit=excluded.unit,
98	       pack_size=excluded.pack_size,
99	       on_hand=excluded.on_hand,
100	       threshold=excluded.threshold,
101	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
102	    [row.barcode, row.name, row.category, row.unit, row.pack_size, row.on_hand, row.threshold, now()]
103	  );
104	}
105	
106	/**
107	 * Replace the local stock cache with whatever the remote returned.
108	 * Upsert every remote row, then drop locally-cached rows the remote
109	 * no longer knows about. Prevents stale items lingering after an admin
110	 * wipes inventory data on the server.
111	 */
112	export async function replaceStockFromRemote(
113	  rows: Array<{
114	    barcode: string;
115	    name: string;
116	    category: string | null;
117	    unit: string | null;
118	    pack_size: number | null;
119	    on_hand: number;
120	    threshold: number;
121	  }>,
122	): Promise<void> {
123	  const db = await getDb();
124	  for (const row of rows) {
125	    await syncStockFromRemote(row);
126	  }
127	  if (rows.length === 0) {
128	    // Remote is empty — wipe local too.
129	    await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_levels`);
130	    return;
131	  }
132	  const ids = rows.map((r) => `'${String(r.barcode).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`).join(',');
133	  await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode NOT IN (${ids})`);
134	}
135	
136	export async function adjustOnHand(barcode: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {
137	  const db = await getDb();
138	  await db.runAsync(
139	    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = MAX(0, on_hand + ?), updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,
140	    [delta, now(), barcode]
141	  );
142	}
143	
144	/**
145	 * Set on_hand and nearest_expiry directly. Used by the "Edit stock"
146	 * correction flow — guard is overwriting the count/expiry, not adding or
147	 * dispensing. Caller is also expected to enqueue a `stock_correction` row
148	 * so the server keeps an audit trail.
149	 */
150	export async function correctStockLocal(
151	  barcode: string,
152	  onHand: number,
153	  expiry: string | null,
154	): Promise<void> {
155	  const db = await getDb();
156	  await db.runAsync(
157	    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = ?, nearest_expiry = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,
158	    [Math.max(0, onHand), expiry, now(), barcode],
159	  );
160	}
161	
162	/** Read the current row's nearest_expiry (TEXT column added in migration 2). */
163	export async function getNearestExpiry(barcode: string): Promise<string | null> {
164	  const db = await getDb();
165	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ nearest_expiry: string | null }>(
166	    `SELECT nearest_expiry FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?`,
167	    [barcode],
168	  );
169	  return row?.nearest_expiry ?? null;
170	}
171

1	/**
2	 * Backend client for the office-ops inventory plugin.
3	 *
4	 * Every request:
5	 *   - is prefixed with `${config.apiBaseUrl}/api/inventory/...`
6	 *   - carries `x-guard-token` (from session) and `x-site-id` / `x-device-id`
7	 *   - throws an `ApiError` with status + body on non-2xx
8	 *
9	 * The outbox flusher (syncService) calls `api.send.*`; screens call
10	 * `api.fetch.*`. When no session token is present, requests still go out
11	 * — the server replies 401 and the outbox keeps the row queued (or
12	 * the read screens fall back to local cache).
13	 */
14	
15	import { config } from '../config';
16	import { clearSession, getSession } from '../auth/session';
17	import { getDeviceId } from '../utils/device';
18	
19	export class ApiError extends Error {
20	  constructor(public status: number, message: string, public body?: string) {
21	    super(message);
22	  }
23	  /** Friendly composite for surfaces that only see `err.message` — e.g.
24	   *  the outbox's `last_error` column. Pulls the server's
25	   *  `{ error: "..." }` body when present so the guard sees a real
26	   *  reason instead of just "HTTP 500". */
27	  static format(status: number, path: string, body: string | undefined): string {
28	    if (body) {
29	      try {
30	        const parsed = JSON.parse(body);
31	        if (parsed && typeof parsed.error === 'string') {
32	          return `HTTP ${status} on ${path} — ${parsed.error}`;
33	        }
34	      } catch {
35	        // not JSON, fall through
36	      }
37	      const trimmed = body.trim();
38	      if (trimmed.length > 0) {
39	        return `HTTP ${status} on ${path} — ${trimmed.slice(0, 180)}`;
40	      }
41	    }
42	    return `HTTP ${status} on ${path}`;
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
47	  const deviceId = await getDeviceId();
48	  const session = getSession();
49	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
50	    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
51	    'X-Device-Id': deviceId,
52	    'X-Site-Id': config.siteId,
53	    ...((init?.headers as Record<string, string>) ?? {}),
54	  };
55	  const sentToken = session?.token ?? null;
56	  if (sentToken) headers['X-Guard-Token'] = sentToken;
57	
58	  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });
59	  if (!res.ok) {
60	    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
61	    const body = text.slice(0, 500);
62	    // 401 with a sent token means the session expired or was revoked
63	    // server-side. Clear it centrally so:
64	    //  - the polling/warm-up loop stops spamming "Invalid or expired
65	    //    session" warnings on every tick,
66	    //  - the AuthProvider's onSessionChange listener fires and the
67	    //    RootNavigator drops back to LoginScreen.
68	    // Skip when no token was sent (the login call itself surfaces 401
69	    // via ApiError to the LoginScreen; there's no session to clear).
70	    if (res.status === 401 && sentToken) {
71	      // Fire-and-forget — clearing is best-effort and shouldn't block
72	      // the error path.
73	      void clearSession().catch(() => {});
74	    }
75	    throw new ApiError(res.status, ApiError.format(res.status, path, body), body);
76	  }
77	  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
78	}
79	
80	const post = (path: string, payload: unknown) =>
81	  request<{ inserted?: boolean; id?: string; barcode?: string }>(path, {
82	    method: 'POST',
83	    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
84	  });
85	
86	export type RemoteOrderItem = {
87	  id: string;
88	  order_id: string;
89	  barcode: string | null;
90	  product_id: string | null;
91	  product_name: string;
92	  expected_qty: number;
93	  received_qty: number;
94	};
95	
96	export type RemoteOrder = {
97	  id: string;
98	  site_id: string;
99	  vendor: string | null;
100	  expected_at: string | null;
101	  arrived_at: string | null;
102	  status: string;
103	  items: RemoteOrderItem[];
104	};
105	
106	export type RemoteStockRow = {
107	  barcode: string;
108	  site_id: string;
109	  name: string;
110	  category: string | null;
111	  unit: string | null;
112	  on_hand: number;
113	  threshold: number;
114	  pack_size: number | null;
115	};
116	
117	export type RemoteProduct = {
118	  barcode: string;
119	  site_id: string;
120	  name: string;
121	  category: string | null;
122	  unit: string | null;
123	  pack_size: number | null;
124	};
125	
126	export type RemoteCatalogHit = {
127	  product: RemoteProduct | null;
128	  openOrderItem: RemoteOrderItem | null;
129	};
130	
131	export type RemoteCanonicalProduct = {
132	  product_id: string;
133	  site_id: string;
134	  canonical_name: string;
135	  category: string | null;
136	  hsn_code: string | null;
137	  unit: string;
138	  pack_size: number;
139	  created_at: string;
140	  updated_at: string;
141	  /** True iff at least one barcode is mapped to this product server-side.
142	   *  Drives the "hide already-taken products" filter when registering a
143	   *  brand-new barcode on the field-app. */
144	  has_barcode?: boolean;
145	  /** Most-recently learned real barcode mapped to this product. Lets the
146	   *  "no barcode" → catalog pick flow reuse the existing mapping instead
147	   *  of synthesising a `catalog_*` id. Null when no barcode is mapped. */
148	  primary_barcode?: string | null;
149	};
150	
151	export const api = {
152	  // Login is handled by visitor's guard auth endpoint, not the inventory
153	  // plugin. Lives here so screens have one client to import.
154	  async login(input: { guardName: string; pin: string }): Promise<{
155	    token: string;
156	    guardId: string;
157	    guardName: string;
158	    language: 'hindi' | 'english';
159	  }> {
160	    const deviceId = await getDeviceId();
161	    const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/api/guard/login`, {
162	      method: 'POST',
163	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
164	      body: JSON.stringify({
165	        guardName: input.guardName,
166	        pin: input.pin,
167	        deviceId,
168	        deviceName: 'field-app',
169	      }),
170	    });
171	    if (!res.ok) {
172	      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
173	      // Pull the server's reason out of `{ "error": "..." }` if present so
174	      // the LoginScreen shows something actionable instead of just "Login failed".
175	      let reason = text.slice(0, 200);
176	      try {
177	        const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
178	        if (parsed && typeof parsed.error === 'string') reason = parsed.error;
179	      } catch {}
180	      throw new ApiError(res.status, reason || `Login failed (${res.status})`, text.slice(0, 500));
181	    }
182	    return res.json();
183	  },
184	
185	  // Writes (outbox-dispatched).
186	  send: {
187	    receipt: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/receipts', payload),
188	    product: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/products', payload),
189	    issue: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/issues', payload),
190	    reorderRequest: (payload: object) =>
191	      post('/api/inventory/reorder-requests', payload),
192	    mismatchFlag: (payload: object) =>
193	      post('/api/inventory/mismatch-flags', payload),
194	    learnBarcode: (payload: object) =>
195	      post('/api/inventory/canonical-products/learn-barcode', payload),
196	    stockCorrection: (payload: object) =>
197	      post('/api/inventory/corrections', payload),
198	  },
199	
200	  // Reads (called by screens).
201	  fetch: {
202	    orders: () =>
203	      request<{ orders: RemoteOrder[] }>('/api/inventory/orders').then((r) => r.orders),
204	    stock: () =>
205	      request<{ stock: RemoteStockRow[] }>('/api/inventory/stock').then((r) => r.stock),
206	    alerts: () =>
207	      request<{ alerts: RemoteStockRow[] }>('/api/inventory/alerts').then((r) => r.alerts),
208	    catalog: (barcode: string) =>
209	      request<RemoteCatalogHit>(
210	        `/api/inventory/catalog/${encodeURIComponent(barcode)}`,
211	      ),
212	    normalizeName: (rawName: string) =>
213	      request<{
214	        canonical: string;
215	        language: 'en' | 'hi' | 'hinglish' | 'unknown';
216	        confidence: number;
217	        cached: boolean;
218	      }>('/api/inventory/products/normalize', {
219	        method: 'POST',
220	        body: JSON.stringify({ rawName }),
221	      }),
222	    canonicalProducts: () =>
223	      request<{ products: RemoteCanonicalProduct[] }>(
224	        '/api/inventory/canonical-products',
225	      ).then((r) => r.products),
226	    /** Single-row catalog read with live-computed `has_barcode` and
227	     *  `primary_barcode`. Used by the catalog picker's JIT verification
228	     *  right before navigating to ReceivingScreen, so the receipt is
229	     *  built against the freshest server state (catches barcode
230	     *  mappings learned by another guard / admin / bill flow that the
231	     *  periodic sync hadn't pulled yet). */
232	    canonicalProduct: (productId: string) =>
233	      request<{ product: RemoteCanonicalProduct }>(
234	        `/api/inventory/canonical-products/${encodeURIComponent(productId)}`,
235	      ).then((r) => r.product),
236	    /** Single-row stock read with nearest future expiry. Used by
237	     *  EditStock and Dispense JIT verification so the user is editing
238	     *  / dispensing against the authoritative on-hand. 404 if the
239	     *  barcode has no server-side stock row yet — caller treats that
240	     *  as "use the local view" rather than as an error. */
241	    stockOne: (barcode: string) =>
242	      request<{ stock: RemoteStockRow & { nearest_expiry: string | null } }>(
243	        `/api/inventory/stock/${encodeURIComponent(barcode)}`,
244	      ).then((r) => r.stock),
245	  },
246	};
247

407	/**

413	  siteId: string,

429	      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.barcode = ?
430	      LIMIT 1
431	    `,
432	    args: [siteId, barcode],
433	  });
434	  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryStockJitRow | undefined) ?? null;
435	}

23	
24	/** Subtle background tint per stock health */
25	function cardBg(row: StockRow): string {
26	  const s = statusFor(row);
27	  if (s === 'out') return 'rgba(229, 57, 53, 0.07)';     // very faint red
28	  if (s === 'low') return 'rgba(249, 168, 37, 0.07)';     // very faint amber
29	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return 'rgba(67, 160, 71, 0.06)'; // faint green
30	  return 'rgba(129, 199, 132, 0.06)';                      // faint light green
31	}
32	
33	/** Count text color per stock health */
34	function countColor(row: StockRow): string {
35	  const s = statusFor(row);
36	  if (s === 'out') return '#E53935';
37	  if (s === 'low') return '#F9A825';
38	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return '#43A047';
39	  return '#66BB6A';
40	}
41	
42	/** Format the pack-size hint that sits under the on-hand count.
43	 *  pack_size null/1 → just the unit ("g"); otherwise "× 100 g". */
44	function packSizeLabel(row: StockRow): string {
45	  const unit = row.unit ?? '';
46	  if (row.pack_size == null || row.pack_size === 1) return unit;
47	  // Trim trailing zero on whole numbers (100 not 100.0) but keep "1.5".
48	  const ps = Number.isInteger(row.pack_size) ? String(row.pack_size) : String(row.pack_size);
49	  return unit ? `× ${ps} ${unit}` : `× ${ps}`;
50	}
51	
52	const STATUS_OPTIONS: FilterOption[] = [
53	  { key: 'All', label: 'All Status' },
54	  { key: 'In Stock', label: 'In Stock' },
55	  { key: 'Low', label: 'Low' },
56	  { key: 'Out', label: 'Out of Stock' },
57	];
58	
59	export function StockScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
60	  const t = useT();
61	  const { palette } = useTheme();
62	  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);
63	  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
64	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
65	  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All');
66	  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
67	  const stockStatus = useCacheStatus('stock');
68	  // Skeleton only while we have NEVER successfully warmed the stock
69	  // cache this session AND we're not currently sitting on a known
70	  // failure state (error / offline) — in those terminal states we'd
71	  // rather show whatever's in local SQLite than a forever-spinner.
72	  const showInitialSkeleton =
73	    !stockStatus.hasEverBeenWarm &&
74	    stockStatus.state !== 'error' &&
75	    stockStatus.state !== 'offline';
76	
77	  const categoryOptions = useMemo<FilterOption[]>(() => {
78	    const cats = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.category).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
79	    return [{ key: 'All', label: 'All Categories' }, ...Array.from(cats).sort().map((c) => ({ key: c, label: c }))];
80	  }, [rows]);
81	
82	  // Pull the latest stock from the server and replace the local cache.
83	  // Throws on failure so refetchThrottled() can flip the cache state to
84	  // error / offline appropriately. flushOnce() runs first so any
85	  // just-queued local writes hit the server before this read.
86	  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {
87	    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
88	    const remote = await api.fetch.stock();
89	    await replaceStockFromRemote(
90	      remote.map((r) => ({
91	        barcode: r.barcode,
92	        name: r.name,
93	        category: r.category,
94	        unit: r.unit,
95	        pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,
96	        on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),
97	        threshold: Number(r.threshold),
98	      })),
99	    );
100	  }, []);
101	
102	  const readLocal = useCallback(async () => {
103	    setRows(await listStock());
104	  }, []);
105	
106	  // Always refetch on focus; the throttle in refetchThrottled stops a
107	  // back-tap burst from firing N parallel requests.
108	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
109	    await refetchThrottled('stock', fetchRemote);
110	    await readLocal();
111	  }, [fetchRemote, readLocal]);
112	
113	  useEffect(() => {
114	    void readLocal();
115	    void refresh();
116	    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => { void refresh(); });
117	    // Subscribe to cache state changes too — warmCache or
118	    // post-write invalidation in flushOnce can update the cache from
119	    // outside this screen; re-read SQLite so the list reflects it.
120	    const unsubCache = onCacheStateChange('stock', (s) => {
121	      if (s.state === 'warm') void readLocal();
122	    });
123	    return () => { unsub(); unsubCache(); };
124	  }, [navigation, refresh, readLocal]);
125	
126	  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
127	    // Pull-to-refresh is a user-initiated request — never skip it.
128	    invalidateRefetchThrottle('stock');
129	    setRefreshing(true);
130	    try {
131	      await refresh();
132	    } finally {
133	      setRefreshing(false);
134	    }
135	  }, [refresh]);
136	
137	  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
138	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
139	    return rows.filter((r) => {
140	      if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;
141	      if (statusFilter !== 'All') {
142	        const s = statusFor(r);
143	        if (statusFilter === 'In Stock' && s !== 'ok') return false;
144	        if (statusFilter === 'Low' && s !== 'low') return false;
145	        if (statusFilter === 'Out' && s !== 'out') return false;
146	      }
147	      if (!q) return true;
148	      return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q) || (r.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
149	    });
150	  }, [rows, query, category, statusFilter]);
151	
152	  return (
153	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
154	      <AppBar title={t('stock')} subtitle={t('stockSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
155	      <KeyboardAvoidingView
156	        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
157	        style={{ flex: 1 }}
158	      >
159	        <View style={styles.search}>
160	        <TextInput
161	          value={query}
162	          onChangeText={setQuery}
163	          placeholder={t('search')}
164	          placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
165	          style={[
166	            styles.searchInput,
167	            {
168	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
169	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
170	              color: palette.onSurface,
171	            },
172	          ]}
173	        />
174	      </View>
175	
176	      {/* Dropdown filter row */}
177	      <View style={styles.filterRow}>
178	        <FilterDropdown
179	          label={t('filterCategory')}
180	          options={categoryOptions}
181	          selected={category}
182	          onSelect={setCategory}
183	        />
184	        <FilterDropdown
185	          label={t('filterStatus')}
186	          options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
187	          selected={statusFilter}
188	          onSelect={setStatusFilter}
189	        />
190	      </View>
191	
192	      {showInitialSkeleton && rows.length === 0 ? (
193	        <View style={styles.list}>
194	          {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
195	            <View key={i} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
196	              <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
197	                <View style={styles.row}>
198	                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 6 }}>
199	                    <Skeleton width="60%" height={18} />
200	                    <Skeleton width="40%" height={14} />
201	                  </View>
202	                  <Skeleton width={40} height={24} />
203	                </View>
204	              </Card>
205	            </View>
206	          ))}
207	        </View>
208	      ) : (
209	      <FlatList
210	        data={filtered}
211	        keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}
212	        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
213	        refreshControl={
214	          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} tintColor={palette.primary} />
215	        }
216	        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}
217	        renderItem={({ item }) => (
218	          <Card
219	            tone="filled"
220	            padding="lg"
221	            style={{ backgroundColor: cardBg(item) }}
222	            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('EditStock', { barcode: item.barcode })}
223	          >
224	            <View style={styles.row}>
225	              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
226	                <Text variant="titleMedium">{item.name}</Text>
227	                <Text
228	                  variant="bodyMedium"
229	                  color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
230	                  style={{ marginTop: 2 }}
231	                >
232	                  {item.category ?? '—'}
233	                </Text>
234	              </View>
235	              <View style={styles.countCol}>
236	                <Text variant="titleLarge" color={countColor(item)} style={{ fontWeight: '700' }}>
237	                  {item.on_hand}
238	                </Text>
239	                {packSizeLabel(item) ? (
240	                  <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
241	                    {packSizeLabel(item)}
242	                  </Text>
243	                ) : null}
244	              </View>
245	            </View>
246	          </Card>
247	        )}
248	        ListEmptyComponent={
249	          <View style={styles.empty}>
250	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
251	              Nothing matches
252	            </Text>
253	            <Text
254	              variant="bodyLarge"
255	              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
256	              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}
257	            >
258	              Try a different filter or clear the search.
259	            </Text>
260	          </View>
261	        }
262	      />
263	      )}
264	      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
265	    </View>
266	  );
267	}
268

270	  safe: { flex: 1 },
271	  search: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.sm },

1	import * as Network from 'expo-network';
2	import { config } from '../config';
3	import { OutboxKind } from '../db/outbox';
4	import { markFailed, markSending, markSent, nextBatch, pendingCount, recoverOrphanedSending } from '../db/outbox';
5	import { replaceCanonicalProducts, type CanonicalProduct } from '../db/catalog';
6	import { upsertOrdersFromRemote } from '../db/orders';
7	import { replaceStockFromRemote } from '../db/stock';
8	import { api } from './api';
9	import { getSession } from '../auth/session';
10	import { cache, type CacheKey } from './cacheStatus';
11	import { invalidateRefetchThrottle } from './refetch';
12	
13	type Listener = (count: number, lastSyncAt: number | null) => void;
14	
15	let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
16	let running = false;
17	let retryQueued = false;
18	let _lastSyncAt: number | null = null;
19	const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
20	
21	export function getLastSyncAt(): number | null {
22	  return _lastSyncAt;
23	}
24	
25	export function onPendingChange(l: Listener): () => void {
26	  listeners.add(l);
27	  pendingCount().then((c) => l(c, _lastSyncAt)).catch(() => {});
28	  return () => listeners.delete(l);
29	}
30	
31	async function notify() {
32	  const c = await pendingCount().catch(() => 0);
33	  listeners.forEach((l) => l(c, _lastSyncAt));
34	}
35	
36	// Dispatch table for outbox kinds. Adding a new kind = one line here
37	// + a handler in api.send.*.
38	const DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {
39	  receipt: api.send.receipt,
40	  product_registration: api.send.product,
41	  issue: api.send.issue,
42	  dispense: api.send.issue, // same endpoint, renamed in-app
43	  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,
44	  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,
45	  learn_barcode: api.send.learnBarcode,
46	  stock_correction: api.send.stockCorrection,
47	};
48	
49	// Per-kind cache invalidation map. After a successful dispatch we
50	// refetch the listed caches so the local view reflects the server's
51	// truth instead of just the optimistic write — closes the lag where
52	// "I just dispensed, but the Stock list still shows the old count".
53	// Empty list = no read-side cache touched (e.g. learn_barcode is
54	// internal plumbing, no user-visible refresh needed).
55	const POST_WRITE_INVALIDATES: Record<OutboxKind, CacheKey[]> = {
56	  receipt: ['stock', 'orders'],
57	  product_registration: ['catalog'],
58	  issue: ['stock', 'alerts'],
59	  dispense: ['stock', 'alerts'],
60	  reorder_request: ['alerts'],
61	  mismatch_flag: ['orders'],
62	  learn_barcode: [],
63	  stock_correction: ['stock', 'alerts'],
64	};
65	
66	/**
67	 * Pull the freshest read-side data for each touched cache. Called once
68	 * per flushOnce after the outbox batch settles. Each fetch runs in
69	 * parallel and updates its cache state independently so a stock pull
70	 * failing doesn't block an orders pull.
71	 */
72	async function invalidateAffectedCaches(keys: Set<CacheKey>): Promise<void> {
73	  const work: Promise<void>[] = [];
74	  for (const key of keys) {
75	    invalidateRefetchThrottle(key);
76	    work.push(refreshOne(key));
77	  }
78	  await Promise.allSettled(work);
79	}
80	
81	async function refreshOne(key: CacheKey): Promise<void> {
82	  cache.refreshing(key);
83	  try {
84	    if (key === 'stock' || key === 'alerts') {
85	      const remote = await api.fetch.stock();
86	      await replaceStockFromRemote(
87	        remote.map((r) => ({
88	          barcode: r.barcode,
89	          name: r.name,
90	          category: r.category,
91	          unit: r.unit,
92	          pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,
93	          on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),
94	          threshold: Number(r.threshold),
95	        })),
96	      );
97	    } else if (key === 'orders') {
98	      await syncOrders();
99	    } else if (key === 'catalog') {
100	      await syncCanonicalProducts();
101	    }
102	    cache.warm(key);
103	  } catch (err) {
104	    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
105	    if (
106	      msg.includes('Network request failed') ||
107	      msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||
108	      msg.includes('Unable to resolve host')
109	    ) {
110	      cache.offline(key);
111	    } else {
112	      cache.error(key);
113	    }
114	  }
115	}
116	
117	export async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
118	  if (running) {
119	    // Queue a retry so manual "Sync Now" taps aren't silently dropped.
120	    retryQueued = true;
121	    return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };
122	  }
123	  running = true;
124	  let sent = 0;
125	  let failed = 0;
126	  try {
127	    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));
128	    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };
129	
130	    // Recover anything orphaned by a prior crash/kill before picking
131	    // the batch — otherwise the stuck row will be skipped again.
132	    await recoverOrphanedSending().catch(() => 0);
133	
134	    // Track which read-side caches got touched by this batch so we can
135	    // refresh them once, after the loop. Refetching per-row would
136	    // multiply network calls on a delivery scan (50 receipts → 50 stock
137	    // pulls); coalescing here means at most one pull per cache per batch.
138	    const touched = new Set<CacheKey>();
139	
140	    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);
141	    for (const row of batch) {
142	      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];
143	      if (!dispatch) {
144	        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);
145	        failed++;
146	        continue;
147	      }
148	      try {
149	        await markSending(row.id);
150	        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));
151	        await markSent(row.id);
152	        sent++;
153	        for (const k of POST_WRITE_INVALIDATES[row.kind]) touched.add(k);
154	      } catch (err) {
155	        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
156	        failed++;
157	      }
158	    }
159	    if (sent > 0 || failed > 0) {
160	      _lastSyncAt = Date.now();
161	    }
162	
163	    // Post-write cache refresh — runs only when at least one write
164	    // committed AND we're still logged in. Bypasses the throttle so the
165	    // user sees their write reflected immediately, without waiting for
166	    // the next periodic timer tick.
167	    if (touched.size > 0 && getSession()?.token) {
168	      await invalidateAffectedCaches(touched);
169	    }
170	
171	    // Skip pulls when not logged in — they'd 401 and only add log noise.
172	    // Login screen / AuthProvider will trigger a flush after a successful login.
173	    if (getSession()?.token) {
174	      // Pull latest canonical products catalog on each sync cycle.
175	      // This is lightweight (just a GET) and ensures guards always
176	      // have the latest admin-curated product names.
177	      await syncCanonicalProducts().catch(() => {});
178	      // Also mirror the open-orders list so ReceivingScreen can resolve
179	      // catalog picks to the right order item without a round-trip.
180	      await syncOrders().catch(() => {});
181	    }
182	  } finally {
183	    running = false;
184	    await notify();
185	  }
186	
187	  // If someone tapped "Sync Now" while we were busy, run again.
188	  if (retryQueued) {
189	    retryQueued = false;
190	    return flushOnce();
191	  }
192	
193	  return { sent, failed };
194	}
195	
196	/**
197	 * Pull the canonical product catalog from the backend and replace
198	 * the local SQLite cache. Guards pick from this list — keeping it
199	 * fresh is essential for the catalog-only gate flow.
200	 *
201	 * Returns the counts so the picker UI can surface "X of Y synced" and
202	 * the user can detect cache mismatches without having to dig into logs.
203	 */
204	let _lastCatalogSync: { remote: number; written: number; failed: number; error: string | null } = {
205	  remote: 0, written: 0, failed: 0, error: null,
206	};
207	export function getLastCatalogSync() { return _lastCatalogSync; }
208	
209	export async function syncCanonicalProducts(): Promise<{ remote: number; written: number; failed: number }> {
210	  try {
211	    const remote = await api.fetch.canonicalProducts();
212	    const local: CanonicalProduct[] = remote.map((r) => ({
213	      product_id: r.product_id,
214	      canonical_name: r.canonical_name,
215	      category: r.category,
216	      hsn_code: r.hsn_code,
217	      unit: r.unit,
218	      pack_size: r.pack_size,
219	      has_barcode: r.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,
220	      primary_barcode: r.primary_barcode ?? null,
221	      updated_at: Date.now(),
222	    }));
223	    const result = await replaceCanonicalProducts(local);
224	    _lastCatalogSync = {
225	      remote: remote.length,
226	      written: result.ok,
227	      failed: result.failed,
228	      error: result.firstError ?? null,
229	    };
230	    if (result.failed > 0) {
231	      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
232	      console.warn(`[catalog] sync wrote ${result.ok}/${remote.length}; ${result.failed} failed. First error: ${result.firstError}`);
233	    }
234	    return { remote: remote.length, written: result.ok, failed: result.failed };
235	  } catch (err) {
236	    _lastCatalogSync = {
237	      ..._lastCatalogSync,
238	      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
239	    };
240	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
241	    console.warn('[catalog] sync failed:', err);
242	    return { remote: 0, written: 0, failed: 0 };
243	  }
244	}
245	
246	/**
247	 * Pull the current open-orders list from the backend and mirror it into
248	 * the local orders/order_items tables. Lets ReceivingScreen resolve a
249	 * catalog pick to the right order item locally.
250	 */
251	export async function syncOrders(): Promise<void> {
252	  try {
253	    const remote = await api.fetch.orders();
254	    await upsertOrdersFromRemote(remote);
255	  } catch {
256	    // Non-fatal: ReceivingScreen falls back to "no matching order".
257	  }
258	}
259	
260	let netSub: { remove: () => void } | null = null;
261	let wasOnline = true;
262	
263	export function startSync(): void {
264	  if (timer) return;
265	  // Recover anything left mid-flight by a previous process before the
266	  // first flush, otherwise nextBatch skips them and they stay stuck on
267	  // "Sending…" forever.
268	  recoverOrphanedSending()
269	    .then((n) => {
270	      if (n > 0) {
271	        // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
272	        console.log(`[sync] recovered ${n} orphaned 'sending' row${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
273	      }
274	    })
275	    .catch(() => {})
276	    .finally(() => {
277	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
278	    });
279	  timer = setInterval(() => {
280	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
281	  }, config.syncIntervalMs);
282	
283	  // Trigger a flush the moment connectivity returns, instead of waiting
284	  // up to syncIntervalMs for the next timer tick. This makes "I just
285	  // walked out from a dead zone" reliably catch up in ~1s.
286	  try {
287	    netSub = Network.addNetworkStateListener((state) => {
288	      const online = !!state.isConnected;
289	      if (online && !wasOnline) {
290	        // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
291	        console.log('[sync] connectivity returned — triggering flush');
292	        flushOnce().catch(() => {});
293	      }
294	      wasOnline = online;
295	    });
296	  } catch {
297	    // Listener API not available on this platform / SDK version; the
298	    // periodic timer still picks things up.
299	  }
300	}
301	
302	export function stopSync(): void {
303	  if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
304	  timer = null;
305	  if (netSub) {
306	    try { netSub.remove(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
307	    netSub = null;
308	  }
309	}
310

351	// Pack size lives on canonical_products (or the older inventory_products
352	// fallback) — not on inventory_stock. Join so the field-app can render
353	// "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" instead of just "{on_hand} {unit}".
354	const STOCK_SELECT = `
355	  s.barcode, s.site_id, s.name, s.category, s.unit, s.on_hand, s.threshold, s.updated_at,
356	  COALESCE(cp.pack_size, ip.pack_size) AS pack_size
357	`;
358	const STOCK_JOINS = `
359	  FROM inventory_stock s
360	  LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode
361	  LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id
362	  LEFT JOIN inventory_products ip ON ip.site_id = s.site_id AND ip.barcode = s.barcode
363	`;
364	
365	export async function listStock(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {
366	  const db = await getDb();
367	  const result = await db.execute({
368	    sql: `SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT} ${STOCK_JOINS} WHERE s.site_id = ? ORDER BY s.name`,
369	    args: [siteId],
370	  });
371	  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];
372	}
373	
374	export async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {
375	  const db = await getDb();
376	  const result = await db.execute({
377	    sql: `
378	      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT}
379	      ${STOCK_JOINS}
380	      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.on_hand <= s.threshold
381	      ORDER BY s.on_hand ASC, s.name ASC
382	    `,
383	    args: [siteId],
384	  });
385	  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];
386	}

2080	function complianceRowToSheetValues(r: ComplianceLogRow): string[] {

2103	    s(r.status),
2104	  ];

1500	 * Snapshot of current stock for the Google Sheet "Stock" tab and the
1501	 * dashboard Stock view. One row per canonical product that has an
1502	 * inventory_stock entry; pulls the most recent unit_rate as price
1503	 * (same source as Catalog) and aggregates distinct *future* expiry
1504	 * dates from receipts (already-passed expiries are dropped — the
1505	 * item is logically consumed/gone).
1506	 */
1507	export async function listStockForSheet(siteId: string): Promise<StockSheetRow[]> {
1508	  const db = await getDb();
1509	  const result = await db.execute({
1510	    sql: `
1511	      SELECT
1512	        COALESCE(cp.canonical_name, s.name) AS canonical_name,
1513	        COALESCE(cp.category, s.category)   AS category,
1514	        COALESCE(cp.unit, s.unit, 'pcs')    AS unit,
1515	        COALESCE(cp.pack_size, 1)           AS pack_size,
1516	        (SELECT string_agg(pb2.barcode, ', ')
1517	           FROM product_barcodes pb2
1518	          WHERE pb2.site_id = s.site_id
1519	            AND pb2.product_id = cp.product_id) AS barcodes,
1520	        (SELECT oi.unit_rate
1521	           FROM inventory_order_items oi
1522	           JOIN inventory_orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
1523	          WHERE oi.product_id = cp.product_id AND o.site_id = ?
1524	          ORDER BY o.created_at DESC
1525	          LIMIT 1) AS last_price,
1526	        s.on_hand AS on_hand,
1527	        (SELECT string_agg(DISTINCT to_char(r.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'), ', ' ORDER BY to_char(r.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'))
1528	           FROM inventory_receipts r
1529	          WHERE r.site_id = s.site_id
1530	            AND r.expiry_date IS NOT NULL
1531	            AND r.expiry_date >= CURRENT_DATE
1532	            AND (r.product_id = cp.product_id OR r.barcode = s.barcode)) AS expiry_dates
1533	      FROM inventory_stock s
1534	      LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode
1535	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id
1536	      WHERE s.site_id = ?
1537	      ORDER BY canonical_name
1538	    `,
1539	    args: [siteId, siteId],
1540	  });
1541	  return result.rows as unknown as StockSheetRow[];
1542	}

> AGENT

I dug through the field-app and engine. All three issues are real and each has a distinct cause — I haven't changed any code yet, just diagnosed. Let me know which fixes you want me to apply.

## 1. "Takes time / sometimes doesn't update" after save

Race in `field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/EditStockScreen.tsx:270-295`. `submit` does:

```ts
await correctStockLocal(...)        // local SQLite updated ✓
await enqueue('stock_correction',...)
flushOnce().catch(() => {})         // fire-and-forget, NOT awaited
navigation.goBack()                  // immediate
```

The Stock screen's focus listener then runs `refresh()` → `fetchRemote()`, which itself calls `flushOnce()`. But `flushOnce` is single-flighted via the `running` flag (`syncService.ts:117-122`) — the in-flight one from `submit` is still running, so this second call returns `{sent:0}` immediately. `api.fetch.stock()` then hits the server **before** the correction has been POSTed, and `replaceStockFromRemote` overwrites the optimistic local row with stale server data.

Eventually the original `flushOnce` finishes and runs `invalidateAffectedCaches(['stock'])` (`syncService.ts:55-78`), which finally pulls correct values and the `onCacheStateChange('warm')` subscription in StockScreen re-reads SQLite. That delay is the "takes time" symptom. If the POST or the follow-up fetch errors (e.g. flaky network), no second invalidate fires and the list stays on the stale value until the next focus/pull-to-refresh — that's the "sometimes doesn't update".

**Simplest fix:** `await flushOnce()` in `submit` before `goBack` (button already shows a loading state via `saving`). Stronger fix: have `replaceStockFromRemote` skip barcodes that have a pending `stock_correction` in the outbox.

## 2. "1g instead of 1 × 200 g"

Not a data bug — display label bug. The data is correct: `on_hand=1` means 1 pack, with `pack_size=200`, `unit=g`. The Stock list (`StockScreen.tsx:236-243`) renders this correctly: big "1", small "× 200 g" beneath.

`EditStockScreen.tsx:384-386` ignores pack_size entirely:

```ts
{t('currentCount')}: {originalQty}{row.unit ? ` ${row.unit}` : ''}
```

So it concatenates "1" + " g" = "1 g". Same bug on the conflict banner at line 348-354. Fix: render like StockScreen — pack hint separate, or "1 pack (× 200 g)".

## 3. Expiry not visible in admin dashboard

This is a server-side gap, not a sync bug. `POST /api/inventory/corrections` (`office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/routes.ts:708-768`):

- Writes `new_expiry` / `new_expiries` into the **audit table** `inventory_stock_corrections` ✓
- Calls `setStockOnHand` (updates `on_hand` only)
- **Never writes the expiry into `inventory_receipts` or onto the stock row**

The admin Stock view's `expiry_dates` column comes from `listStockForSheet` (`db.ts:1507-1542`), which aggregates `inventory_receipts.expiry_date` — corrections are invisible to it. Same with the field-app's JIT `nearest_expiry` in `findStockJit` (`db.ts:418-427`). `db.ts:402` even has the acknowledgement comment: *"Best-effort — corrections do not feed back into this today"*.

The field-app shows the new expiry only because `correctStockLocal` writes it into local `stock_levels.nearest_expiry`. Nothing server-side surfaces it.

**Cleanest fix:** add `nearest_expiry DATE` to `inventory_stock`, have `setStockOnHand` (or a sibling) write it from the correction payload, and have `listStockForSheet` + `findStockJit` prefer that column over the receipts aggregate when set. Heavier alternative: insert a synthetic receipt on correction.

---

Want me to apply (1) + (2) on the field-app and (3) on the engine in one pass, or pick a subset?

Undefined leaves the column untouched. */\n  nearestExpiry?: string | null;\n  fallbackName?: string;\n  fallbackCategory?: string | null;\n  fallbackUnit?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const touchExpiry = input.nearestExpiry !== undefined;\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold, nearest_expiry)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, GREATEST(?, 0), 0, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n        on_hand = GREATEST(?, 0),\n        nearest_expiry = ${touchExpiry ? '?' : 'inventory_stock.nearest_expiry'},\n        updated_at = now()\n    `,\n    args: touchExpiry\n      ? [\n          input.barcode,\n          input.siteId,\n          input.fallbackName ?? \"Unknown\",\n          input.fallbackCategory ?? null,\n          input.fallbackUnit ?? null,\n          input.onHand,\n          input.nearestExpiry,\n          input.onHand,\n          input.nearestExpiry,\n        ]\n      : [\n          input.barcode,\n          input.siteId,\n          input.fallbackName ?? \"Unknown\",\n          input.fallbackCategory ?? null,\n          input.fallbackUnit ?? null,\n          input.onHand,\n          null,\n          input.onHand,\n        ],\n  });\n}"
}

> AGENT

All three fixes applied; TS clean (the remaining engine errors are pre-existing in `visitor/routes.ts`, unrelated).

**field-app** — `EditStockScreen.tsx`:
- `submit` now `await`s `flushOnce()` before `goBack` — closes the race where StockScreen's focus refetch ran before the POST and overwrote the optimistic write.
- Added a `countSuffix` helper that drops the unit when `pack_size != 1` (the count is in packs, not grams). Used by the "current count" line and the conflict banner — no more "1 g" for a 200 g pack.

**office-ops-engine** — corrections now persist server-side:
- `postgres-schema.ts`: `ALTER TABLE inventory_stock ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS nearest_expiry DATE` (idempotent).
- `db.ts`: `setStockOnHand` accepts `nearestExpiry?: string | null` (undefined = leave, null = clear, string = set). `findStockJit` and `listStockForSheet` now `COALESCE(s.nearest_expiry, receipts-aggregate)` so the guard-typed expiry takes priority but still falls back to the receipt-derived one.
- `routes.ts`: corrections handler computes `effectiveExpiry` (earliest of `new_expiries`, else `new_expiry`, else null) and passes it through. Admin dashboard's Stock view will now show the guard-set expiry.

Not committed. Worth testing the flow end-to-end on the device since the EditStock screen + the stock-snapshot query both changed.

> DEVELOPER

we need to solve the expiry issue, suppose we have multiple packets of an item, 10 packets for example, and 3
  packets have same expiry, 4 have same but differ from the other 3 packets, and rest 3 paackets have same
  expiry date, but different from the previous 2 groups, now if we choose different expiry option in the
  field-app the user would have to put 10 different expiry. isn't that a bad UX. also for dispense also we need
  to track expiry, suppose a user picks 3 from these 10, and all three are one from each group, so 1 decrease
  from the all three different expiry group, how would the user be able to mark them and how would system store
  them and show them.

> AGENT

You're right — the per-pack toggle was a half-measure. Forcing 10 individual rows when reality is 3 groups is exactly the kind of friction warehouse staff won't tolerate. And dispense currently has no expiry awareness at all, so once it's mixed pack reality, the data is fiction.

The fix is a data model change, not a UI tweak: **track expiry batches (lots), not per-pack dates.** This is the standard inventory pattern (FEFO — first-expired-first-out) and it collapses every scenario the user described into the same shape.

## Proposed model

New table `inventory_stock_lots`:

```
(site_id, barcode, lot_id, expiry_date NULL, qty NUMERIC, source, created_at)
```

`inventory_stock.on_hand` = `SUM(lots.qty)` for that barcode. `nearest_expiry` becomes `MIN(lots.expiry_date WHERE qty > 0)` — kept as a cached column on `inventory_stock` so existing reads (admin dashboard, field-app list) need no change.

Your 10-pack example is **3 rows**, not 10:
```
{qty:3, expiry:2026-08-15}
{qty:4, expiry:2026-09-10}
{qty:3, expiry:2026-10-01}
```

## UX changes

**Receiving / EditStock — replace "per-pack" toggle with "batches":**

```
Total: 10 packs
  ┌────────────────────────────┐
  │ 3 packs · 15/08/2026   [x] │
  │ 4 packs · 10/09/2026   [x] │
  │ 3 packs · 01/10/2026   [x] │
  │ + Add another expiry batch │
  └────────────────────────────┘
```

Same widget for receive and edit. One batch = same expiry, by default. Tap "+" only when groups differ. Solves the 10-pack UX directly.

**Dispense — FEFO default, override only when needed:**

For the 90% case (1 pack at a time): guard scans, system silently decrements the earliest-expiry lot. Shows "Took 1 from batch exp. 15/08/2026" as confirmation. Zero extra taps.

For the picked-3-from-3-groups case: after entering qty=3, screen shows lots ranked by expiry:
```
Pick from:
  [3] 15/08/2026  (3 in stock)  ← suggested (FEFO)
  [ ] 10/09/2026  (4 in stock)
  [ ] 01/10/2026  (3 in stock)
```
Default highlights "take 3 from earliest". Guard adjusts to `[1] [1] [1]` if reality differed. One tap if FEFO matches reality, three taps if not.

**Admin dashboard — stock list:**

`Expiry dates` column already comma-separates dates. Now becomes "3@15/08, 4@10/09, 3@01/10" — staff can see distribution without drilling in.

## Cost / tradeoff frame (for the Ameya conversation)

- **One-time:** new table + migrate existing `nearest_expiry` to a single lot per row; refactor Receiving + EditStock + Dispense screens. ~2 days dev.
- **Recurring:** zero — same Postgres, same field-app, no new services.
- **Cheap non-tech alternative:** keep the manual marker-on-pack practice with FEFO discipline. Falls apart at scale (which is the entire reason this app exists), but no recurring cost.
- **Risk if we don't:** dispense expiry tracking can't exist meaningfully without lots — on-hand drifts from physical the moment one expiry "group" runs out and the next group's date should kick in.

## Open question I'd want a call on

**Do we enforce FEFO or just record what staff actually did?**

- Enforce → guard cannot pick from newer batch while older exists. Accounting always matches the policy. Risk: real life has damaged/missing packs and staff route around it.
- Record → guard freely picks any batch. Accounting matches physical. Risk: guards casually pick newest, expiries silently accumulate.

I'd default to **record with a soft nudge** (the "← suggested" hint), because the constraint here is staff literacy, not policy compliance — and we can build the FEFO-violation alert later if pattern analysis warrants it.

Want me to implement this? It's a meaningful change touching schema, field-app, and dashboard, so I'd suggest one focused PR for the data model + receiving/edit, and a second for dispense — but I can do it all in one if you prefer.

1	/**
2	 * Inventory Plugin — HTTP Routes
3	 *
4	 * Field-app facing surface under /api/inventory/*.
5	 *
6	 * Writes (idempotent on `id`):
7	 *   POST /api/inventory/receipts          — gate capture
8	 *   POST /api/inventory/products          — catalog upsert (register barcode)
9	 *   POST /api/inventory/issues            — consumption
10	 *   POST /api/inventory/reorder-requests  — low-stock reorder ask
11	 *   POST /api/inventory/mismatch-flags    — receipt mismatch report
12	 *
13	 * Reads (guard-authenticated):
14	 *   GET  /api/inventory/orders            — open orders + items
15	 *   GET  /api/inventory/stock             — all stock rows
16	 *   GET  /api/inventory/alerts            — low + out of stock
17	 *   GET  /api/inventory/catalog/:barcode  — product lookup
18	 *
19	 * Auth: reuses the visitor module's guard session token. The field-app
20	 * obtains it via POST /api/guard/login (visitor routes) and sends it as
21	 * the `x-guard-token` header. Site selection is done with the
22	 * `x-site-id` header (defaults to the guard's session site when added).
23	 */

453	    const id = asString(body.id);

473	    // Fallback: if the client couldn't resolve an order_item_id but did

476	    // counter stays at 0 whenever the field-app's local order cache
477	    // hasn't caught up to the latest validated bill.
478	    if (!orderItemId && productId) {
479	      const openItem = await findOpenItemByProductId(auth.siteId, productId);
480	      if (openItem) orderItemId = openItem.id;
481	    }

483	    const result = await insertReceipt({

498	
499	    // Only mutate downstream state on the first successful insert.

512	        fallbackUnit: existing?.unit ?? null,
513	      });
514	      // Defense in depth: persist the barcode → canonical_product mapping

521	          barcode,
522	          productId,
523	          learnedFrom: "scan",
524	        }).catch(() => {});
525	        // Catalog tab on the linked Sheet needs to reflect the freshly-

545	    return true;

602	      void syncCatalogTabToSheet(auth.siteId).catch((err) => {

3	let dbInstance: SQLite.SQLiteDatabase | null = null;
4	
5	/**
6	 * Current schema version. Bump this and add a migration block in
7	 * `runMigrations()` whenever you need additive schema changes.
8	 */
9	const SCHEMA_VERSION = 7;

11	export async function getDb(): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {
12	  if (dbInstance) return dbInstance;
13	  const db = await SQLite.openDatabaseAsync('fieldapp.db');
14	  await db.execAsync(`
15	    PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
16	    PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
17	
18	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS products (
19	      barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
20	      name TEXT NOT NULL,
21	      category TEXT,
22	      unit TEXT,
23	      pack_size REAL,
24	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
25	    );
26	
27	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
28	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
29	      vendor TEXT,
30	      expected_at INTEGER,
31	      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
32	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
33	    );
34	
35	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_items (
36	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
37	      order_id TEXT NOT NULL,
38	      barcode TEXT,
39	      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
40	      expected_qty REAL NOT NULL,
41	      received_qty REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
42	      FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
43	    );
44	
45	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS receipts (
46	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
47	      order_id TEXT,
48	      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
49	      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
50	      qty REAL NOT NULL,
51	      scanned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
52	      note TEXT
53	    );
54	
55	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
56	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
57	      kind TEXT NOT NULL,

61	      last_error TEXT,
62	      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,

64	    );
65	
66	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbox_status ON outbox(status);
67	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_order ON order_items(order_id);
68	
69	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_levels (
70	      barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
71	      name TEXT NOT NULL,
72	      category TEXT,
73	      unit TEXT,
74	      pack_size REAL,
75	      on_hand REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
76	      threshold REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
77	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
78	    );
79	
80	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_stock_category ON stock_levels(category);
81	  `);

88	/**
89	 * Forward-only migrations keyed by PRAGMA user_version. Each migration
90	 * runs once; version is bumped after success. ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN is
91	 * idempotent in SQLite (errors if column exists), so we guard with the
92	 * version check rather than catching errors.
93	 */
94	async function runMigrations(db: SQLite.SQLiteDatabase): Promise<void> {
95	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>(
96	    'PRAGMA user_version',
97	  );
98	  let version = row?.user_version ?? 0;
99	
100	  if (version < 1) {
101	    await db.execAsync(`
102	      -- Per-scan expiry and audit columns on receipts
103	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN expiry_date TEXT;
104	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by TEXT;
105	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by_name TEXT;
106	
107	      -- Audit trail table
108	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_log (
109	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
110	        action TEXT NOT NULL,
111	        barcode TEXT,
112	        qty REAL,
113	        expiry_date TEXT,
114	        performed_by TEXT,
115	        performed_by_name TEXT,
116	        performed_at INTEGER NOT NULL
117	      );
118	
119	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_action ON activity_log(action);
120	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_barcode ON activity_log(barcode);
121	
122	      PRAGMA user_version = 1;
123	    `);
124	    version = 1;
125	  }
126	
127	  // Future migrations go here:
128	  // if (version < 3) { ... PRAGMA user_version = 3; }
129	
130	  if (version < 2) {
131	    await db.execAsync(`
132	      -- Track nearest expiry per stock item for quick alert queries
133	      ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN nearest_expiry TEXT;
134	
135	      -- Per-receipt expiry tracking. Each received item with an expiry date
136	      -- gets a row here. This lets us query "what's expiring in the next N days"
137	      -- and trace it back to the order/receipt it came from.
138	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expiry_tracking (
139	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
140	        barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
141	        product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
142	        expiry_date TEXT NOT NULL,
143	        qty REAL NOT NULL,
144	        receipt_id TEXT,
145	        received_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
146	        performed_by TEXT,
147	        performed_by_name TEXT
148	      );
149	
150	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_barcode ON expiry_tracking(barcode);
151	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_date ON expiry_tracking(expiry_date);
152	
153	      PRAGMA user_version = 2;
154	    `);
155	    version = 2;
156	  }
157	
158	  if (version < 3) {
159	    await db.execAsync(`
160	      -- Canonical product catalog (synced from backend).
161	      -- Guards pick from this list — no free-text product names.
162	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS canonical_products (
163	        product_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
164	        canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,
165	        category TEXT,
166	        hsn_code TEXT,
167	        unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pcs',
168	        pack_size REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
169	        has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
170	        updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
171	      );
172	
173	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_name ON canonical_products(canonical_name);
174	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_hsn ON canonical_products(hsn_code);
175	
176	      -- Learned barcode → canonical product mappings.
177	      -- Populated when guard scans a barcode + picks from catalog.
178	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product_barcodes (
179	        barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
180	        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
181	        learned_from TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'scan',
182	        learned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
183	        FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES canonical_products(product_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
184	      );
185	
186	      -- Link order items to canonical products for name-based matching
187	      ALTER TABLE order_items ADD COLUMN product_id TEXT;
188	
189	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_product ON order_items(product_id);
190	
191	      PRAGMA user_version = 3;
192	    `);
193	    version = 3;
194	  }
195	
196	  if (version < 4) {
197	    // Fresh installs already have pack_size from the initial CREATE TABLE;
198	    // only older DBs (created before the column was added to the base schema)
199	    // need the ALTER. Probe table_info to decide.
200	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
201	      'PRAGMA table_info(products)',
202	    );
203	    const hasPackSize = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'pack_size');
204	    if (!hasPackSize) {
205	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE products ADD COLUMN pack_size REAL;`);
206	    }
207	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 4;`);
208	    version = 4;
209	  }
210	
211	  if (version < 5) {
212	    // Mirror the canonical product's pack_size onto stock so the Stock
213	    // screen can render "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" without an extra
214	    // join at read time.
215	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
216	      'PRAGMA table_info(stock_levels)',
217	    );
218	    const hasPackSize = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'pack_size');
219	    if (!hasPackSize) {
220	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN pack_size REAL;`);
221	    }
222	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 5;`);
223	    version = 5;
224	  }
225	
226	  if (version < 6) {
227	    // Track whether each canonical product already has a barcode mapped
228	    // server-side. The register-new-barcode suggestion list filters these
229	    // out so two barcodes can't map to the same product (current rule).
230	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
231	      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',
232	    );
233	    const hasBarcodeCol = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'has_barcode');
234	    if (!hasBarcodeCol) {
235	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`);
236	    }
237	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 6;`);
238	    version = 6;
239	  }
240	
241	  if (version < 7) {
242	    // Cache the canonical product's primary (most-recent) barcode locally
243	    // so the "No barcode → catalog pick" path can reuse an existing
244	    // mapping created by another guard / admin / bill upload without a
245	    // round-trip. Without this, the device only knows barcodes IT has
246	    // learned, so a server-side mapping invisible here forces the synthetic
247	    // `catalog_*` id and the "No barcode yet" chip on the receiving screen.
248	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
249	      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',
250	    );
251	    const hasPrimary = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'primary_barcode');
252	    if (!hasPrimary) {
253	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN primary_barcode TEXT;`);
254	    }
255	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 7;`);
256	    version = 7;
257	  }
258	}
259	
260	export function now(): number {
261	  return Date.now();
262	}
