36	/** Format ISO date to DD/MM/YYYY for display */
37	function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {
38	  try {
39	    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
40	    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
41	  } catch {
42	    return iso;
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	/** Convert Date object to ISO date string */
47	function toISO(d: Date): string {
48	  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
49	}
50	
51	const QUICK_MONTHS = [
52	  { label: '3 mo', months: 3 },
53	  { label: '6 mo', months: 6 },
54	  { label: '1 yr', months: 12 },
55	  { label: '2 yr', months: 24 },
56	];
57	
58	export function ReceivingScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
59	  const t = useT();
60	  const { palette } = useTheme();
61	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
62	  const { barcode, productId, productName: paramProductName, unit: paramUnit, packSize: paramPackSize } = route.params;
63	  const orderSession = useOrderSession();
64	  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);
65	  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);
66	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
67	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
68	
69	  // Expiry date state — driven by native date picker
70	  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);
71	  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState(false);
72	  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);
73	  // Per-pack expiry. Default OFF — most bulk scans share one batch /
74	  // one expiry. When ON, expand a list of N rows so the guard can set
75	  // a different expiry on each pack of the multi-quantity scan.
76	  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);
77	  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);
78	  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
79	
80	  // Keep the per-pack list in lockstep with qty when the toggle is on.
81	  // Initialise new rows with the bulk expiry so the guard can edit
82	  // only the ones that actually differ.
83	  useEffect(() => {
84	    if (!perPackExpiry) return;
85	    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {
86	      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));
87	      if (prev.length === n) return prev;
88	      const next = [...prev];
89	      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);
90	      while (next.length > n) next.pop();
91	      return next;
92	    });
93	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);
94	
95	  // Resolved product name: canonical name takes priority over legacy product name
96	  const resolvedName = paramProductName ?? product?.name ?? 'Unknown';
97	  const resolvedUnit = paramUnit ?? product?.unit ?? null;
98	  const resolvedPackSize = paramPackSize ?? null;
99	  const packDisplay =
100	    resolvedPackSize != null && resolvedUnit
101	      ? `${resolvedPackSize} ${resolvedUnit}`
102	      : resolvedUnit ?? null;
103	
104	  useEffect(() => {
105	    (async () => {
106	      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));
107	      // If we have a productId from catalog, look up order item by product_id
108	      if (productId) {
109	        const byProduct = await findOpenItemByProductId(productId);
110	        setItem(byProduct as OrderItem | null);
111	      } else {
112	        setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));
113	      }
114	    })();
115	  }, [barcode, productId]);
116	
117	  // Auto-fill expiry from last scanned item in the order session
118	  useEffect(() => {
119	    if (orderSession.lastExpiry && !noExpiry && !expiryDateObj) {
120	      setExpiryDateObj(new Date(orderSession.lastExpiry));
121	    }
122	  }, [orderSession.lastExpiry, noExpiry, expiryDateObj]);
123	
124	  const setExact = (n: number) => {
125	    haptic.tap();
126	    setQty(n);
127	  };
128	
129	  const expiryDate = useMemo(() => {
130	    if (noExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;
131	    return toISO(expiryDateObj);
132	  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry]);
133	
134	  const handleQuickMonth = (months: number) => {
135	    haptic.tap();
136	    const d = new Date();
137	    d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + months);
138	    setExpiryDateObj(d);
139	    setNoExpiry(false);
140	  };
141	
142	  const handlePickerChange = (date: Date) => {
143	    setExpiryDateObj(date);
144	    setNoExpiry(false);
145	  };
146	
147	  /** Standalone receipt path — when no order session is active */
148	  const persistStandalone = async (flagged: boolean) => {
149	    const session = getSession();
150	    // Per-pack expiry: split into N qty=1 receipts so each pack carries
151	    // its own expiry date. Single-expiry path stays as one bulk row.
152	    const receipts: Array<{ id: string; qty: number; expiry: string | null }> = perPackExpiry
153	      ? perPackExpiries.map((d) => ({
154	          id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`,
155	          qty: 1,
156	          expiry: d ? toISO(d) : null,
157	        }))
158	      : [{ id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`, qty, expiry: expiryDate }];
159	
160	    // The first receipt's id is what we use to link mismatch flags etc.
161	    const id = receipts[0].id;
162	
163	    for (const r of receipts) {
164	      await enqueue('receipt', {
165	        id: r.id,
166	        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,
167	        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,
168	        product_id: productId ?? null,
169	        barcode,
170	        product_name: resolvedName,
171	        qty: r.qty,
172	        expiry_date: r.expiry,
173	        flagged,
174	        scanned_at: Date.now(),
175	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
176	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
177	      });
178	    }
179	
180	    // Barcode learning: if this receipt was resolved via canonical catalog,
181	    // permanently map this barcode to the product_id for auto-resolution.
182	    if (productId && !barcode.startsWith('catalog_')) {
183	      await learnBarcode(barcode, productId, 'scan');
184	      // Also tell the backend to learn it
185	      await enqueue('learn_barcode', {
186	        barcode,
187	        product_id: productId,
188	      }).catch(() => {});
189	    }
190	    if (flagged) {
191	      await enqueue('mismatch_flag', {
192	        id: `flg_${nanoid(12)}`,
193	        receipt_id: id,
194	        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,
195	        barcode,
196	        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,
197	        received_total: (item?.received_qty ?? 0) + qty,
198	        flagged_at: Date.now(),
199	      });
200	    }
201	    if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);
202	
203	    const existing = await findStock(barcode);
204	    if (existing) {
205	      await adjustOnHand(barcode, qty);
206	    } else {
207	      await upsertStock({
208	        barcode,
209	        name: resolvedName,
210	        category: product?.category ?? null,
211	        unit: resolvedUnit,
212	        pack_size: resolvedPackSize,
213	        on_hand: qty,
214	        threshold: 0,
215	      });
216	    }
217	
218	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
219	
220	    // Track expiry for future alerts
221	    if (expiryDate) {
222	      await trackExpiry({
223	        barcode,
224	        productName: resolvedName,
225	        expiryDate,
226	        qty,
227	        receiptId: id,
228	        performedBy: session?.guardId ?? null,
229	        performedByName: session?.guardName ?? null,
230	      });
231	    }
232	  };
233	
234	  /** Add to order session */
235	  const addToSession = () => {
236	    // Per-pack expiry → N qty=1 batches each with its own date.
237	    // Single-expiry → one batch with the full qty.
238	    const batches = perPackExpiry
239	      ? perPackExpiries.map((d) => ({ qty: 1, expiry: d ? toISO(d) : null }))
240	      : [{ qty, expiry: expiryDate }];
241	    const totalQty = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + b.qty, 0);
242	
243	    orderSession.addItem({
244	      barcode,
245	      productId: productId ?? null,
246	      name: resolvedName,
247	      category: product?.category ?? null,
248	      unit: resolvedUnit,
249	      packSize: resolvedPackSize,
250	      qty: totalQty,
251	      batches,
252	    });
253	    haptic.success();
254	    navigation.navigate('OrderSession');
255	  };
256	
257	  /** Standalone confirm (no active order session) */
258	  const confirmStandalone = async () => {
259	    setSaving(true);
260	    try {
261	      await persistStandalone(false);
262	      haptic.success();
263	      navigation.replace('DeliverySummary', {
264	        items: [{ name: resolvedName, category: product?.category ?? null, qty }],
265	        totalItems: 1,
266	        totalQty: qty,
267	        productName: resolvedName,
268	        qty,
269	        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,
270	        flagged: false,
271	      });
272	    } finally {
273	      setSaving(false);
274	    }
275	  };
276	
277	  const flagAndContinueStandalone = async () => {
278	    setSaving(true);
279	    try {
280	      await persistStandalone(true);
281	      haptic.warn();
282	      navigation.replace('DeliverySummary', {
283	        items: [{ name: resolvedName, category: product?.category ?? null, qty }],
284	        totalItems: 1,
285	        totalQty: qty,
286	        productName: resolvedName,
287	        qty,
288	        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,
289	        flagged: true,
290	      });
291	    } finally {
292	      setSaving(false);
293	    }
294	  };
295	
296	  const expected = item?.expected_qty ?? null;
297	  const alreadyIn = item?.received_qty ?? 0;
298	  const projected = alreadyIn + qty;
299	  const mismatch = expected !== null && projected > expected;
300	
301	  return (
302	    <KeyboardAvoidingView
303	      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'height' : undefined}
304	      style={{ flex: 1 }}
305	    >
306	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
307	        <AppBar
308	          title={t('receiveItem')}
309	          onBack={() => navigation.goBack()}
310	          trailing={
311	            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.xs }}>
312	              {expected !== null ? (
313	                <StatusPill
314	                  label={`${projected} / ${expected}`}
315	                  tone={mismatch ? 'danger' : projected === expected ? 'success' : 'neutral'}
316	                />
317	              ) : null}
318	              <IconButton Icon={ScanLine} onPress={() => navigation.replace('Scanner')} />
319	            </View>
320	          }

1	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
2	import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
3	import { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';
4	import { trackExpiry } from '../../../db/expiry';
5	import { adjustOnHand, findStock, upsertStock } from '../../../db/stock';
6	import { findProduct } from '../../../db/products';
7	import { addReceivedQty, findOpenItemByBarcode } from '../../../db/orders';
8	import { findOpenItemByProductId, learnBarcode } from '../../../db/catalog';
9	import { getSession } from '../../../auth/session';
10	import { flushOnce, syncOrders } from '../../../sync/syncService';
11	import { now } from '../../../db/database';
12	
13	export type OrderBatch = {
14	  qty: number;
15	  expiry: string | null; // ISO date, e.g. "2027-03-15"
16	};
17	
18	export type OrderSessionItem = {
19	  barcode: string;
20	  /** Canonical product id when the user picked one from the catalog. Lets
21	   *  the receipt link back to the right order_item and lets us learn the
22	   *  barcode → product mapping for next time. */
23	  productId?: string | null;
24	  name: string;
25	  category: string | null;
26	  unit: string | null;
27	  /** Pack size (unit value) for display: "qty × packSize unit". */
28	  packSize?: number | null;
29	  /** Total qty across all batches. Kept in sync with sum(batches[].qty). */
30	  qty: number;
31	  /** One row per distinct expiry. Single-expiry receipts have one batch;
32	   *  per-pack-expiry receipts have N batches of qty=1. */
33	  batches: OrderBatch[];
34	};
35	
36	type OrderSessionCtx = {
37	  items: OrderSessionItem[];
38	  addItem: (item: OrderSessionItem) => void;
39	  removeItem: (barcode: string) => void;
40	  updateItemQty: (barcode: string, qty: number) => void;
41	  clear: () => void;
42	  submitAll: () => Promise<void>;
43	  isActive: boolean;
44	  /** Last expiry date entered — used for "same as last" auto-fill */
45	  lastExpiry: string | null;
46	};
47	
48	const Ctx = createContext<OrderSessionCtx | null>(null);
49	
50	export function OrderSessionProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
51	  const [items, setItems] = useState<OrderSessionItem[]>([]);
52	  const [lastExpiry, setLastExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);
53	
54	  const addItem = useCallback((item: OrderSessionItem) => {
55	    // "Same as last" auto-fill uses the most recent non-null expiry the
56	    // guard explicitly set, regardless of which batch it came from.
57	    const lastNonNull = [...item.batches].reverse().find((b) => b.expiry)?.expiry;
58	    if (lastNonNull) setLastExpiry(lastNonNull);
59	    setItems((prev) => {
60	      const existing = prev.find((i) => i.barcode === item.barcode);
61	      if (existing) {
62	        // Merge by concatenating batches so distinct expiries survive a
63	        // re-scan of the same product.
64	        return prev.map((i) =>
65	          i.barcode === item.barcode
66	            ? {
67	                ...i,
68	                qty: i.qty + item.qty,
69	                batches: [...i.batches, ...item.batches],
70	              }
71	            : i,
72	        );
73	      }
74	      return [...prev, item];
75	    });
76	  }, []);
77	
78	  const removeItem = useCallback((barcode: string) => {
79	    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((i) => i.barcode !== barcode));
80	  }, []);
81	
82	  const updateItemQty = useCallback((barcode: string, qty: number) => {
83	    // Collapses batches into a single batch carrying the earliest expiry —
84	    // safe default since manual qty edits can't reconcile against multiple
85	    // distinct expiries.
86	    setItems((prev) =>
87	      prev.map((i) => {
88	        if (i.barcode !== barcode) return i;
89	        const earliest = i.batches
90	          .map((b) => b.expiry)
91	          .filter((e): e is string => !!e)
92	          .sort()[0] ?? null;
93	        return { ...i, qty, batches: [{ qty, expiry: earliest }] };
94	      }),
95	    );
96	  }, []);
97	
98	  const clear = useCallback(() => {
99	    setItems([]);
100	    setLastExpiry(null);
101	  }, []);
102	
103	  const submitAll = useCallback(async () => {
104	    const session = getSession();
105	    const performedBy = session?.guardId ?? null;
106	    const performedByName = session?.guardName ?? null;
107	
108	    // Make sure our local mirror of open orders is current before we try
109	    // to resolve productId → order_item_id. Without this, the very first
110	    // scan of the day misses the order link and the Received counter
111	    // stays at 0 until the next periodic sync.
112	    await syncOrders().catch(() => {});
113	
114	    for (const item of items) {
115	      // Prefer the canonical-product link (set when guard picked from the
116	      // catalog); fall back to barcode lookup for legacy barcoded items.
117	      const orderItem = item.productId
118	        ? await findOpenItemByProductId(item.productId).catch(() => null)
119	        : await findOpenItemByBarcode(item.barcode).catch(() => null);
120	
121	      // One receipt per batch so each distinct expiry survives end-to-end
122	      // (outbox → backend → dashboard → Excel).
123	      const batches = item.batches.length > 0 ? item.batches : [{ qty: item.qty, expiry: null }];
124	      for (const batch of batches) {
125	        const receiptId = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;
126	        await enqueue('receipt', {
127	          id: receiptId,
128	          order_id: orderItem?.order_id ?? null,
129	          order_item_id: orderItem?.id ?? null,
130	          product_id: item.productId ?? null,
131	          barcode: item.barcode,
132	          product_name: item.name,
133	          qty: batch.qty,
134	          expiry_date: batch.expiry,
135	          flagged: false,
136	          scanned_at: now(),
137	          performed_by: performedBy,
138	          performed_by_name: performedByName,
139	        });
140	
141	        if (batch.expiry) {
142	          await trackExpiry({
143	            barcode: item.barcode,
144	            productName: item.name,
145	            expiryDate: batch.expiry,
146	            qty: batch.qty,
147	            receiptId,
148	            performedBy,
149	            performedByName,
150	          });
151	        }
152	      }
153	
154	      // Update local order tracking if applicable (once per item — uses summed qty)
155	      if (orderItem) {
156	        await addReceivedQty(orderItem.id, item.qty);
157	      }
158	
159	      // Barcode learning — remember this real barcode → canonical product
160	      // so the next scan auto-resolves to the right item without the
161	      // CatalogPicker. Skip synthetic "catalog_*" barcodes from picks
162	      // with no physical barcode.
163	      if (item.productId && !item.barcode.startsWith('catalog_')) {
164	        await learnBarcode(item.barcode, item.productId, 'scan');
165	        await enqueue('learn_barcode', {
166	          barcode: item.barcode,
167	          product_id: item.productId,
168	        }).catch(() => {});
169	      }
170	
171	      // Roll qty into on-hand stock (once per item — total qty across batches)
172	      const existing = await findStock(item.barcode);
173	      if (existing) {
174	        await adjustOnHand(item.barcode, item.qty);
175	      } else {
176	        await upsertStock({
177	          barcode: item.barcode,
178	          name: item.name,
179	          category: item.category,
180	          unit: item.unit,
181	          pack_size: item.packSize ?? null,
182	          on_hand: item.qty,
183	          threshold: 0,
184	        });
185	      }
186	    }
187	
188	    // Try to sync immediately, don't block if offline
189	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
190	
191	    // Clear session after successful submission
192	    setItems([]);
193	    setLastExpiry(null);
194	  }, [items]);
195	
196	  const value = useMemo<OrderSessionCtx>(
197	    () => ({
198	      items,
199	      addItem,
200	      removeItem,
201	      updateItemQty,
202	      clear,
203	      submitAll,
204	      isActive: items.length > 0,
205	      lastExpiry,
206	    }),
207	    [items, addItem, removeItem, updateItemQty, clear, submitAll, lastExpiry],
208	  );
209	
210	  return <Ctx.Provider value={value}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
211	}
212	
213	export function useOrderSession(): OrderSessionCtx {
214	  const ctx = useContext(Ctx);
215	  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useOrderSession must be inside OrderSessionProvider');
216	  return ctx;
217	}
218

44	  { key: 'In Stock', label: 'In Stock' },
45	  { key: 'Low', label: 'Low' },
46	  { key: 'Out', label: 'Out of Stock' },
47	];
48	
49	type VerifyStatus = 'verifying' | 'ok' | 'absent' | 'offline' | 'error';
50	
51	function isOfflineError(err: unknown): boolean {
52	  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
53	  return (
54	    msg.includes('Network request failed') ||
55	    msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||
56	    msg.includes('Unable to resolve host')
57	  );
58	}
59	
60	/** Subtle background tint per stock health */
61	function cardBg(row: StockRow): string {
62	  const s = statusFor(row);
63	  if (s === 'out') return 'rgba(229, 57, 53, 0.07)';
64	  if (s === 'low') return 'rgba(249, 168, 37, 0.07)';
65	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return 'rgba(67, 160, 71, 0.06)';
66	  return 'rgba(129, 199, 132, 0.06)';
67	}
68	
69	/** Count text color per stock health */
70	function countColor(row: StockRow): string {
71	  const s = statusFor(row);
72	  if (s === 'out') return '#E53935';
73	  if (s === 'low') return '#F9A825';
74	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return '#43A047';
75	  return '#66BB6A';
76	}
77	
78	export function DispenseScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
79	  const t = useT();
80	  const { palette } = useTheme();
81	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
82	  const initialBarcode = route.params?.barcode;
83	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);
84	  const [all, setAll] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);
85	  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
86	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
87	  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All');
88	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
89	  const [reason, setReason] = useState(REASONS[0]);
90	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
91	  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);
92	
93	  // JIT verification — fetch the server's authoritative on_hand the
94	  // moment a product is selected. Dispense MUST commit against the
95	  // freshest count so we never let a guard dispense 10 from a stock
96	  // of 8. Fail-closed: if the JIT fetch fails or times out, the
97	  // confirm button stays disabled until the user goes back and tries
98	  // again with network.
99	  const [verifyStatus, setVerifyStatus] = useState<VerifyStatus>('verifying');
100	  const [verifiedOnHand, setVerifiedOnHand] = useState<number | null>(null);
101	
102	  const categoryOptions = useMemo<FilterOption[]>(() => {
103	    const cats = new Set(all.map((r) => r.category).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
104	    return [{ key: 'All', label: 'All Categories' }, ...Array.from(cats).sort().map((c) => ({ key: c, label: c }))];
105	  }, [all]);
106	
107	  useEffect(() => {
108	    listStock().then((rows) => {
109	      setAll(rows);
110	      setInitialLoading(false);
111	    });
112	  }, []);
113	
114	  useEffect(() => {
115	    if (initialBarcode) {
116	      findStock(initialBarcode).then((r) => r && setSelected(r));
117	    }
118	  }, [initialBarcode]);
119	
120	  // Whenever an item gets selected, re-run JIT verification. The
121	  // dependency on `selected?.barcode` (not the whole row) means
122	  // refetching only happens for an actual product change, not for the
123	  // optimistic update we apply after submit.
124	  useEffect(() => {
125	    if (!selected) {
126	      setVerifyStatus('verifying');
127	      setVerifiedOnHand(null);
128	      return;
129	    }
130	    let cancelled = false;
131	    setVerifyStatus('verifying');
132	    setVerifiedOnHand(null);
133	    const controller = new AbortController();
134	    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);
135	    (async () => {
136	      try {
137	        const remote = await api.fetch.stockOne(selected.barcode);
138	        if (cancelled) return;
139	        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);
140	        setVerifiedOnHand(remoteQty);
141	        setVerifyStatus('ok');
142	        // Clamp qty to the verified ceiling. If the user had a stale
143	        // higher qty in mind (e.g. local cache said 10, server says 7),
144	        // drop to the new max so they can't accidentally submit over.
145	        if (qty > remoteQty) setQty(Math.max(1, remoteQty));
146	      } catch (err) {
147	        if (cancelled) return;
148	        // 404: the server has no record of this barcode (might be a
149	        // device-only item never received server-side). Treat as
150	        // "absent" — block dispense; you can't dispense what the
151	        // server doesn't know exists.
152	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
153	          setVerifyStatus('absent');
154	          return;
155	        }
156	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;
157	        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');
158	      } finally {
159	        clearTimeout(t);
160	      }
161	    })();
162	    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };
163	    // intentionally NOT in deps: `qty` would re-run the JIT on every
164	    // stepper tap; only refire when the selected barcode changes.
165	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
166	  }, [selected?.barcode]);
167	
168	  const results = useMemo(() => {
169	    if (selected) return [];
170	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
171	    return all
172	      .filter((r) => {
173	        if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;
174	        if (statusFilter !== 'All') {
175	          const s = statusFor(r);
176	          if (statusFilter === 'In Stock' && s !== 'ok') return false;
177	          if (statusFilter === 'Low' && s !== 'low') return false;
178	          if (statusFilter === 'Out' && s !== 'out') return false;
179	        }
180	        if (!q) return true;
181	        return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q) || (r.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
182	      })
183	      .slice(0, 30);
184	  }, [all, query, selected, category, statusFilter]);
185	
186	  const submit = async () => {
187	    if (!selected) return;
188	    const session = getSession();
189	    setSaving(true);
190	    try {
191	      await enqueue('dispense', {
192	        id: `dsp_${nanoid(12)}`,
193	        barcode: selected.barcode,
194	        product_name: selected.name,
195	        qty,
196	        reason,
197	        // taken_by is now always the logged-in guard — no separate
198	        // free-text "who" field. Removes one mandatory input from the
199	        // dispense flow.
200	        taken_by: session?.guardName ?? null,
201	        issued_at: Date.now(),
202	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
203	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
204	      });
205	      await adjustOnHand(selected.barcode, -qty);
206	      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
207	      haptic.success();
208	      navigation.goBack();
209	    } finally {
210	      setSaving(false);
211	    }
212	  };
213	
214	  if (!selected) {
215	    return (
216	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
217	        <AppBar title={t('dispense')} subtitle={t('dispenseSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
218	        <View style={styles.search}>
219	          <TextInput
220	            value={query}
221	            onChangeText={setQuery}
222	            placeholder={t('search')}
223	            placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
224	            style={[
225	            styles.searchInput,
226	            {
227	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
228	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
229	              color: palette.onSurface,
230	            },
231	          ]}
232	            autoFocus
233	          />
234	        </View>
235	        <View style={styles.filterRow}>
236	          <FilterDropdown
237	            label={t('filterCategory')}
238	            options={categoryOptions}
239	            selected={category}
240	            onSelect={setCategory}
241	          />
242	          <FilterDropdown
243	            label={t('filterStatus')}
244	            options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
245	            selected={statusFilter}
246	            onSelect={setStatusFilter}
247	          />
248	        </View>
249	        {initialLoading && all.length === 0 ? (
250	          <View style={styles.list}>
251	            {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
252	              <View key={i} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
253	                <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
254	                  <Skeleton width="55%" height={18} />
255	                  <View style={{ height: 6 }} />
256	                  <Skeleton width="75%" height={14} />
257	                </Card>
258	              </View>
259	            ))}
260	          </View>
261	        ) : (
262	          <FlatList
263	            data={results}
264	            keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}
265	            contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
266	            ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}
267	            renderItem={({ item }) => (
268	              <Card tone="filled" padding="lg" onPress={() => setSelected(item)} style={{ backgroundColor: cardBg(item) }}>
269	                <View style={styles.pickerRow}>
270	                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
271	                    <Text variant="titleMedium">{item.name}</Text>
272	                    <Text
273	                      variant="bodyMedium"
274	                      color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
275	                      style={{ marginTop: 2 }}
276	                    >
277	                      {item.category ?? '—'}
278	                    </Text>
279	                  </View>
280	                  <Text variant="titleLarge" color={countColor(item)} style={{ fontWeight: '700' }}>
281	                    {item.on_hand}
282	                  </Text>
283	                </View>
284	              </Card>
285	            )}
286	            ListEmptyComponent={
287	              <View style={styles.empty}>
288	                <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
289	                  No items match.
290	                </Text>
291	              </View>
292	            }
293	          />
294	        )}
295	      </View>
296	    );
297	  }
298	
299	  return (
300	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
301	      <AppBar
302	        title={t('dispense')}
303	        subtitle={selected.name}
304	        onBack={() => setSelected(null)}
305	      />
306	      <KeyboardAvoidingView
307	        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
308	        style={{ flex: 1 }}
309	      >
310	        <ScrollView
311	          contentContainerStyle={[styles.body, { paddingBottom: spacing.xl + kbHeight }]}
312	          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
313	        >
314	          {/* JIT verification banners. While verifying, the user can
315	              still scroll and pick a reason — they just can't confirm
316	              the dispense yet. */}
317	          {verifyStatus === 'verifying' && (
318	            <View style={styles.statusBanner}>
319	              <ActivityIndicator color={palette.primary} size="small" />
320	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm }}>
321	                {t('verifying')}
322	              </Text>
323	            </View>
324	          )}
325	          {(verifyStatus === 'offline' || verifyStatus === 'error' || verifyStatus === 'absent') && (
326	            <View
327	              style={[
328	                styles.statusBanner,
329	                { borderColor: palette.error, backgroundColor: palette.errorContainer ?? 'transparent' },
330	              ]}
331	            >
332	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.error}>
333	                {t('couldntVerify')}
334	              </Text>
335	            </View>
336	          )}
337	
338	          <Card tone="elevated" padding="xl">
339	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
340	              PRODUCT
341	            </Text>
342	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
343	              {selected.name}
344	            </Text>
345	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
346	              {/* Show the verified on_hand once JIT resolves; fall back
347	                  to the local cache value until then. Per pack/unit
348	                  formatting unchanged. */}
349	              {verifiedOnHand ?? selected.on_hand}
350	              {selected.pack_size != null && selected.pack_size !== 1 ? ` × ${selected.pack_size}` : ''}
351	              {selected.unit ? ` ${selected.unit}` : ''} {t('onHand')}
352	            </Text>
353	          </Card>
354	
355	          <View style={styles.qtySection}>
356	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
357	              {t('howMany').toUpperCase()}
358	            </Text>
359	            {/* Max is the JIT-verified count when we have it; otherwise
360	                the cached value. Stepper itself enforces the ceiling. */}
361	            <QtyStepper
362	              value={qty}
363	              onChange={setQty}
364	              min={1}
365	              max={(verifiedOnHand ?? selected.on_hand) || undefined}
366	            />
367	          </View>
368	
369	          <View>
370	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
371	              {t('reason').toUpperCase()}
372	            </Text>
373	            <View style={styles.chipRow}>
374	              {REASONS.map((r) => (
375	                <Chip key={r} label={r} selected={r === reason} onPress={() => setReason(r)} />
376	              ))}
377	            </View>
378	          </View>
379	
380	          {/* Who took it: always the logged-in guard. No need for a
381	              text input; just surface their name so they know the
382	              record will be filed under them. */}
383	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
384	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
385	              {t('whoTook').toUpperCase()}
386	            </Text>
387	            <Text variant="titleMedium" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
388	              {getSession()?.guardName ?? '—'}
389	            </Text>
390	          </View>
391	        </ScrollView>
392	
393	        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>
394	          {/* Confirm is gated by verification — fail-closed per the
395	              cache-freshness plan. While verifying or after a failed
396	              verification, the user can adjust qty / reason but can't
397	              commit until they reach a verified state. */}
398	          <Button
399	            label={t('confirm')}
400	            onPress={submit}
401	            loading={saving}
402	            disabled={verifyStatus !== 'ok'}
403	            size="lg"
404	            fullWidth
405	            leadingIcon={<Check size={22} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
406	          />
407	        </View>
408	      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
409	    </View>
410	  );
411	}
412	
413	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
414	  safe: { flex: 1 },
415	  search: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.sm },
416	  filterRow: {
417	    flexDirection: 'row',
418	    gap: spacing.sm,
419	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,
420	    paddingVertical: spacing.md,
421	  },
422	  searchInput: {
423	    borderRadius: radius.md,
424	    borderWidth: 1.5,
425	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,
426	    minHeight: 52,
427	    fontSize: 17,

One row per (site, barcode, expiry)\n    -- carrying the remaining qty in that batch. Receipts insert/merge\n    -- here, dispenses decrement (FEFO by default), corrections replace\n    -- the entire set for a barcode. SUM(qty) over lots == inventory_stock.on_hand\n    -- by construction; the column is kept in sync as a cached total.\n    -- NULL expiry means \"no expiry\" (e.g. hardware, non-perishable);\n    -- treated as a regular lot that sorts last in FEFO.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_stock_lots (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      expiry_date DATE,\n      qty NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n      source TEXT,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n      updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n    -- A barcode can have at most one open lot per expiry — receipts with\n    -- the same expiry are merged into that row. NULL expiries collapse\n    -- to a single \"no-expiry\" lot too via COALESCE in the upsert path.\n    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_inv_stock_lots_site_barcode_expiry\n      ON inventory_stock_lots(site_id, barcode, COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01'));\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_stock_lots_fefo\n      ON inventory_stock_lots(site_id, barcode, expiry_date NULLS LAST);"

1	/**
2	 * Inventory Plugin — Database Layer
3	 *
4	 * SQL surface backing the field-app gate-entry flow. Distinct from the
5	 * legacy top-level `inventory` table, which stores LLM extractions from
6	 * chat messages — these tables persist the structured capture stream
7	 * (products, orders, receipts, stock, issues, reorder requests,
8	 * mismatch flags).
9	 *
10	 * Schema migration runs from `lib/postgres-schema.ts` via
11	 * `ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema()`, the same boot path the visitor
12	 * module uses. This file owns the CRUD only.
13	 */

{

  "old_string": "// ─── Canonical Products ──────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "// ─── Stock Lots (per-expiry batches) ──────────────────────────────\n\nexport type StockLot = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null means \"no expiry\". */\n  expiry_date: string | null;\n  qty: number;\n  source: string | null;\n  created_at: string;\n  updated_at: string;\n};\n\n/** Compact lot shape for API responses + FEFO planning. */\nexport type LotSummary = {\n  expiry_date: string | null;\n  qty: number;\n};\n\n/** List remaining lots for a barcode, FEFO-ordered (earliest first,\n *  null-expiry last). Used by the field-app's dispense picker and the\n *  JIT stock fetch. */\nexport async function listLots(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<LotSummary[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT to_char(expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS expiry_date, qty\n      FROM inventory_stock_lots\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0\n      ORDER BY expiry_date NULLS LAST\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  return result.rows.map((r) => ({\n    expiry_date: (r as { expiry_date: string | null }).expiry_date,\n    qty: Number((r as { qty: number | string }).qty),\n  }));\n}\n\n/** Recompute the denormalised cache columns (on_hand, nearest_expiry)\n *  on inventory_stock from the current lot rows. Call after any lot\n *  mutation. Cheap — one indexed scan + one update. */\nasync function refreshStockCache(siteId: string, barcode: string): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      UPDATE inventory_stock SET\n        on_hand = COALESCE((\n          SELECT SUM(qty) FROM inventory_stock_lots\n          WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0\n        ), 0),\n        nearest_expiry = (\n          SELECT MIN(expiry_date) FROM inventory_stock_lots\n          WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0 AND expiry_date IS NOT NULL\n        ),\n        updated_at = now()\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, barcode, siteId, barcode, siteId, barcode],\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Add qty to the lot identified by (site, barcode, expiry). Inserts a\n * new lot row if none exists, otherwise increments the existing one.\n * Called by the receipts handler. Refreshes the cached on_hand /\n * nearest_expiry on inventory_stock for read-side consumers.\n */\nexport async function addLot(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  expiryDate: string | null;\n  qty: number;\n  source?: string;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  if (input.qty <= 0) return;\n  const db = await getDb();\n  // We can't use ON CONFLICT with COALESCE expression keys reliably\n  // across drivers, so do find-or-insert. Race window is tiny; the\n  // unique index serialises any contention.\n  const existing = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT id, qty FROM inventory_stock_lots\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?\n        AND COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01') = COALESCE(?::DATE, DATE '0001-01-01')\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [input.siteId, input.barcode, input.expiryDate],\n  });\n  const row = existing.rows[0] as { id: string; qty: number | string } | undefined;\n  if (row) {\n    await db.execute({\n      sql: `UPDATE inventory_stock_lots SET qty = qty + ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ?`,\n      args: [input.qty, row.id],\n    });\n  } else {\n    await db.execute({\n      sql: `\n        INSERT INTO inventory_stock_lots (id, site_id, barcode, expiry_date, qty, source)\n        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      `,\n      args: [\n        `lot_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,\n        input.siteId,\n        input.barcode,\n        input.expiryDate,\n        input.qty,\n        input.source ?? null,\n      ],\n    });\n  }\n  await refreshStockCache(input.siteId, input.barcode);\n}\n\n/**\n * Replace ALL lots for a (site, barcode) with the given list. Used by\n * the corrections endpoint when the guard re-stated the full per-batch\n * breakdown. Empty array clears all lots (zero stock). Empty/null\n * expiries collapse into a single \"no-expiry\" lot per the unique index.\n */\nexport async function replaceLots(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  lots: LotSummary[];\n  source?: string;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `DELETE FROM inventory_stock_lots WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?`,\n    args: [input.siteId, input.barcode],\n  });\n  // Coalesce duplicates the caller may have sent (two batches with the\n  // same expiry → one merged lot).\n  const merged = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const lot of input.lots) {\n    if (lot.qty <= 0) continue;\n    const key = lot.expiry_date ?? \"__none__\";\n    merged.set(key, (merged.get(key) ?? 0) + lot.qty);\n  }\n  for (const [key, qty] of merged) {\n    const expiry = key === \"__none__\" ? null : key;\n    await db.execute({\n      sql: `\n        INSERT INTO inventory_stock_lots (id, site_id, barcode, expiry_date, qty, source)\n        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      `,\n      args: [\n        `lot_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,\n        input.siteId,\n        input.barcode,\n        expiry,\n        qty,\n        input.source ?? \"correction\",\n      ],\n    });\n  }\n  await refreshStockCache(input.siteId, input.barcode);\n}\n\n/**\n * Subtract qty from lots. When `picks` is provided, decrement those\n * specific (expiry, qty) pairs — the guard explicitly told us which\n * batches they pulled from. When absent, decrement FEFO (oldest expiry\n * first, null expiry last) — the \"soft nudge\" default.\n *\n * Returns the actual breakdown applied so the caller can persist it\n * (audit) and the field-app can update the local mirror.\n *\n * If the total in lots is short of qty, decrements what's there and\n * returns the partial breakdown. We do not let on_hand go negative.\n */\nexport async function decrementLots(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  qty: number;\n  picks?: LotSummary[] | null;\n}): Promise<LotSummary[]> {\n  if (input.qty <= 0) return [];\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const applied: LotSummary[] = [];\n\n  // Path A — explicit picks\n  if (input.picks && input.picks.length > 0) {\n    for (const pick of input.picks) {\n      if (pick.qty <= 0) continue;\n      const row = await db.execute({\n        sql: `\n          SELECT id, qty FROM inventory_stock_lots\n          WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?\n            AND COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01') = COALESCE(?::DATE, DATE '0001-01-01')\n            AND qty > 0\n          LIMIT 1\n        `,\n        args: [input.siteId, input.barcode, pick.expiry_date],\n      });\n      const lot = row.rows[0] as { id: string; qty: number | string } | undefined;\n      if (!lot) continue;\n      const available = Number(lot.qty);\n      const take = Math.min(available, pick.qty);\n      if (take <= 0) continue;\n      await db.execute({\n        sql: `UPDATE inventory_stock_lots SET qty = qty - ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ?`,\n        args: [take, lot.id],\n      });\n      applied.push({ expiry_date: pick.expiry_date, qty: take });\n    }\n  } else {\n    // Path B — FEFO\n    let remaining = input.qty;\n    const lots = await db.execute({\n      sql: `\n        SELECT id, to_char(expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS expiry_date, qty\n        FROM inventory_stock_lots\n        WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0\n        ORDER BY expiry_date NULLS LAST\n      `,\n      args: [input.siteId, input.barcode],\n    });\n    for (const r of lots.rows) {\n      if (remaining <= 0) break;\n      const lot = r as { id: string; expiry_date: string | null; qty: number | string };\n      const available = Number(lot.qty);\n      const take = Math.min(available, remaining);\n      await db.execute({\n        sql: `UPDATE inventory_stock_lots SET qty = qty - ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ?`,\n        args: [take, lot.id],\n      });\n      applied.push({ expiry_date: lot.expiry_date, qty: take });\n      remaining -= take;\n    }\n  }\n\n  await refreshStockCache(input.siteId, input.barcode);\n  // Vacuum empty lots so they don't clutter reads. Optional but cheap.\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `DELETE FROM inventory_stock_lots WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty <= 0`,\n    args: [input.siteId, input.barcode],\n  });\n  return applied;\n}\n\n// ─── Canonical Products ──────────────────────────────────────────"
}

Idempotent — no-op if the row already\n * exists, even when the fallback fields differ.\n */\nexport async function ensureStockRow(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  fallbackName?: string;\n  fallbackCategory?: string | null;\n  fallbackUnit?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 0)\n      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.barcode,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.fallbackName ?? \"Unknown\",\n      input.fallbackCategory ?? null,\n      input.fallbackUnit ?? null,\n    ],\n  });\n}\n\n// ─── Stock Lots (per-expiry batches) ──────────────────────────────"

[...newExpiries].sort()[0]\n          : newExpiry ?? null;\n      await setStockOnHand({\n        siteId: auth.siteId,\n        barcode,\n        onHand: newQty,\n        nearestExpiry: effectiveExpiry,\n        fallbackName: productName,\n      });",
  "new_string": "    if (result.inserted) {\n      await ensureStockRow({\n        siteId: auth.siteId,\n        barcode,\n        fallbackName: productName,\n      });\n\n      // The field-app sends either:\n      //   - lots: [{qty, expiry_date}]  (new batch model)\n      //   - new_expiries: [DATE]         (legacy per-pack; one date per unit)\n      //   - new_expiry: DATE             (legacy single)\n      // Convert all three shapes to a unified replaceLots payload.\n      const rawLots = Array.isArray(body.lots) ? body.lots : null;\n      let lotsPayload: LotSummary[];\n      if (rawLots) {\n        lotsPayload = rawLots\n          .map((l) => {\n            const expiry = asString((l as Record<string, unknown>).expiry_date);\n            const lotQty = asNumber((l as Record<string, unknown>).qty);\n            if (lotQty === null || lotQty <= 0) return null;\n            return { expiry_date: expiry, qty: lotQty } as LotSummary;\n          })\n          .filter((l): l is LotSummary => l !== null);\n      } else if (newExpiries && newExpiries.length > 0) {\n        // Legacy per-pack: collapse identical dates into a single lot.\n        const merged = new Map<string, number>();\n        for (const d of newExpiries) merged.set(d, (merged.get(d) ?? 0) + 1);\n        lotsPayload = Array.from(merged.entries()).map(([d, q]) => ({\n          expiry_date: d,\n          qty: q,\n        }));\n      } else {\n        lotsPayload = newQty > 0 ?   "new_string": "export async function findStockJit(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryStockJitRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},\n        COALESCE(\n          to_char(s.nearest_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),\n          (\n            SELECT to_char(MIN(r.expiry_date), 'YYYY-MM-DD')\n            FROM inventory_receipts r\n            WHERE r.site_id = s.site_id\n              AND r.barcode = s.barcode\n              AND r.expiry_date IS NOT NULL\n              AND r.expiry_date >= CURRENT_DATE\n          )\n        ) AS nearest_expiry\n      ${STOCK_JOINS}\n      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.barcode = ?\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  const row = result.rows[0] as\n    | (Omit<InventoryStockJitRow, \"lots\"> & { lots?: undefined })\n    | undefined;\n  if (!row) return null;\n  const lots = await listLots(siteId, barcode);\n  return { ...row, lots } as InventoryStockJitRow;\n}"

1128	    const send = (eventName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>): void => {
1129	      eventId += 1;
1130	      const line =
1131	        `id: ${eventId}\n` +

1141	    // First event fires immediately so a successful connection is
1142	    // visible to the client even if no heartbeats have fired yet.
1143	    send("open", { ts: Date.now(), msg: "smoke stream opened" });
1144	
1145	    // Heartbeat every 10 s. Two roles:
1146	    //   1. Proves the stream is still receiving server-side writes.
1147	    //   2. Keeps proxy idle-timeouts (nginx default 60 s) from killing
1148	    //      the connection on quiet streams.
1149	    const heartbeat = setInterval(() => {
1150	      send("tick", { ts: Date.now(), eventId });
1151	    }, 10_000);
1152	
1153	    // Hard cap so a forgotten browser tab doesn't pin a worker forever.
1154	    // 5 min is long enough to confirm proxy behavior is stable across
1155	    // typical Bifrost idle-timeout windows.
1156	    const maxDurationMs = 5 * 60 * 1000;
1157	    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
1158	      send("close", { ts: Date.now(), reason: "max-duration" });
1159	      try { res.end(); } catch { /* ignore */ }

1161	
1162	    // Client disconnect cleanup. `req.on('close')` fires when the
1163	    // browser/curl drops or the proxy times out. Without this, the
1164	    // interval keeps firing into a dead socket forever.
1165	    const cleanup = () => {
1166	      clearInterval(heartbeat);
1167	      clearTimeout(timeout);

1170	    res.on("close", cleanup);

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function listStock(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT} ${STOCK_JOINS} WHERE s.site_id = ? ORDER BY s.name`,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];\n}",
  "new_string": "export type InventoryStockListRow = InventoryStockRow & {\n  nearest_expiry: string | null;\n  lots: LotSummary[];\n};\n\nexport async function listStock(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockListRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},\n        to_char(s.nearest_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS nearest_expiry,\n        COALESCE(\n          (SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(\n                    'expiry_date', to_char(l.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),\n                    'qty', l.qty)\n                  ORDER BY l.expiry_date NULLS LAST)\n             FROM inventory_stock_lots l\n            WHERE l.site_id = s.site_id AND l.barcode = s.barcode AND l.qty > 0),\n          '[]'::json\n        ) AS lots\n      ${STOCK_JOINS}\n      WHERE s.site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY s.name\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows.map((r) => {\n    const row = r as Record<string, unknown>;\n    // Some Postgres drivers (pg / pglite) return json columns as strings;\n    // others auto-parse. Normalise.\n    let lots = row.lots as LotSummary[] | string | null;\n    if (typeof lots === \"string\") {\n      try { lots = JSON.parse(lots) as LotSummary[]; } catch { lots = []; }\n    }\n    if (!Array.isArray(lots)) lots = [];\n    return {\n      ...(row as InventoryStockRow),\n      nearest_expiry: (row.nearest_expiry as string | null) ?? null,\n      lots: lots.map((l) => ({\n        expiry_date: l.expiry_date ?? null,\n        qty: Number(l.qty),\n      })),\n    } as InventoryStockListRow;\n  });\n}"
}

  "old_string": "  if (version < 7) {\n    // Cache the canonical product's primary (most-recent) barcode locally\n    // so the \"No barcode → catalog pick\" path can reuse an existing\n    // mapping created by another guard / admin / bill upload without a\n    // round-trip. Without this, the device only knows barcodes IT has\n    // learned, so a server-side mapping invisible here forces the synthetic\n    // `catalog_*` id and the \"No barcode yet\" chip on the receiving screen.\n    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(\n      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',\n    );\n    const hasPrimary = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'primary_barcode');\n    if (!hasPrimary) {\n      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN primary_barcode TEXT;`);\n    }\n    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 7;`);\n    version = 7;\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "  if (version < 7) {\n    // Cache the canonical product's primary (most-recent) barcode locally\n    // so the \"No barcode → catalog pick\" path can reuse an existing\n    // mapping created by another guard / admin / bill upload without a\n    // round-trip. Without this, the device only knows barcodes IT has\n    // learned, so a server-side mapping invisible here forces the synthetic\n    // `catalog_*` id and the \"No barcode yet\" chip on the receiving screen.\n    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(\n      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',\n    );\n    const hasPrimary = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'primary_barcode');\n    if (!hasPrimary) {\n      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN primary_barcode TEXT;`);\n    }\n    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 7;`);\n    version = 7;\n  }\n\n  if (version < 8) {\n    // Per-expiry batches (\"lots\"), mirroring the server's\n    // inventory_stock_lots. Each row is \"qty of this barcode that share\n    // this expiry\". Receipts insert/merge by (barcode, expiry); dispenses\n    // decrement FEFO or by explicit picks; corrections replace the set\n    // for a barcode wholesale. NULL expiry = \"no expiry\" — collapses\n    // into a single lot keyed by the sentinel below for uniqueness.\n    await db.execAsync(`\n      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_lots (\n        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n        expiry_date TEXT,\n        qty REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        source TEXT,\n        updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL\n      );\n      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_stock_lots_barcode ON stock_lots(barcode);\n      CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_stock_lots_barcode_expiry\n        ON stock_lots(barcode, COALESCE(expiry_date, '__no_expiry__'));\n      PRAGMA user_version = 8;\n    `);\n    version = 8;\n  }\n}"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/db/lots.ts",
  "content": "import { getDb, now } from './database';\n\nexport type Lot = {\n  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null means \"no expiry\". */\n  expiry_date: string | null;\n  qty: number;\n};\n\n/**\n * List current lots for a barcode, FEFO-ordered (earliest expiry first,\n * NULL last). Used by EditStock to seed the BatchEditor and by Dispense\n * to surface the picker.\n */\nexport async function listLots(barcode: string): Promise<Lot[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<{ expiry_date: string | null; qty: number }>(\n    `SELECT expiry_date, qty FROM stock_lots\n      WHERE barcode = ? AND qty > 0\n      ORDER BY (expiry_date IS NULL), expiry_date ASC`,\n    [barcode],\n  );\n  return rows.map((r) => ({ expiry_date: r.expiry_date, qty: Number(r.qty) }));\n}\n\n/**\n * Replace all lots for a barcode with the given list. Used when the\n * server returns a fresh snapshot (warmCache, post-write refetch) and\n * when EditStock commits a correction.\n */\nexport async function replaceLotsLocal(\n  barcode: string,\n  lots: Lot[],\n): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_lots WHERE barcode = ?`, [barcode]);\n  // Coalesce duplicates so the unique index holds.\n  const merged = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const lot of lots) {\n    if (lot.qty <= 0) continue;\n    const key = lot.expiry_date ?? '__no_expiry__';\n    merged.set(key, (merged.get(key) ?? 0) + Number(lot.qty));\n  }\n  let i = 0;\n  const ts = now();\n  for (const [key, qty] of merged) {\n    const expiry = key === '__no_expiry__' ? null : key;\n    await db.runAsync(\n      `INSERT INTO stock_lots (id, barcode, expiry_date, qty, source, updated_at)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n      [`lot_${ts}_${i++}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6)}`, barcode, expiry, qty, 'sync', ts],\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Add qty to a single lot identified by (barcode, expiry). Used on\n * optimistic write from ReceivingScreen so the BatchEditor reflects\n * the new pack before the server round-trip.\n */\nexport async function addLotLocal(\n  barcode: string,\n  expiry: string | null,\n  qty: number,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (qty <= 0) return;\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const key = expiry ?? '__no_expiry__';\n  const existing = await db.getFirstAsync<{ id: string; qty: number }>(\n    `SELECT id, qty FROM stock_lots\n      WHERE barcode = ? AND COALESCE(expiry_date, '__no_expiry__') = ?\n      LIMIT 1`,\n    [barcode, key],\n  );\n  const ts = now();\n  if (existing) {\n    await db.runAsync(\n      `UPDATE stock_lots SET qty = qty + ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,\n      [qty, ts, existing.id],\n    );\n  } else {\n    await db.runAsync(\n      `INSERT INTO stock_lots (id, barcode, expiry_date, qty, source, updated_at)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n      [`lot_${ts}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6)}`, barcode, expiry, qty, 'receipt', ts],\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Subtract qty from lots. Mirrors the server's decrementLots: explicit\n * picks when provided, FEFO otherwise. Returns the actual breakdown\n * applied so the dispense flow can include it in the outbox payload.\n */\nexport async function decrementLotsLocal(\n  barcode: string,\n  totalQty: number,\n  picks?: Lot[] | null,\n): Promise<Lot[]> {\n  if (totalQty <= 0) return [];\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const applied: Lot[] = [];\n  const ts = now();\n\n  if (picks && picks.length > 0) {\n    for (const pick of picks) {\n      if (pick.qty <= 0) continue;\n      const key = pick.expiry_date ?? '__no_expiry__';\n      const lot = await db.getFirstAsync<{ id: string; qty: number }>(\n        `SELECT id, qty FROM stock_lots\n          WHERE barcode = ? AND COALESCE(expiry_date, '__no_expiry__') = ?\n          LIMIT 1`,\n        [barcode, key],\n      );\n      if (!lot) continue;\n      const take = Math.min(Number(lot.qty), pick.qty);\n      if (take <= 0) continue;\n      await db.runAsync(\n        `UPDATE stock_lots SET qty = qty - ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,\n        [take, ts, lot.id],\n      );\n      applied.push({ expiry_date: pick.expiry_date, qty: take });\n    }\n  } else {\n    let remaining = totalQty;\n    const lots = await db.getAllAsync<{ id: string; expiry_date: string | null; qty: number }>(\n      `SELECT id, expiry_date, qty FROM stock_lots\n        WHERE barcode = ? AND qty > 0\n        ORDER BY (expiry_date IS NULL), expiry_date ASC`,\n      [barcode],\n    );\n    for (const lot of lots) {\n      if (remaining <= 0) break;\n      const take = Math.min(Number(lot.qty), remaining);\n      await db.runAsync(\n        `UPDATE stock_lots SET qty = qty - ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,\n        [take, ts, lot.id],\n      );\n      applied.push({ expiry_date: lot.expiry_date, qty: take });\n      remaining -= take;\n    }\n  }\n\n  await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_lots WHERE barcode = ? AND qty <= 0`, [barcode]);\n  return applied;\n}\n\n/**\n * Drop lots for barcodes the remote no longer knows about. Mirrors the\n * stock-level reaping in replaceStockFromRemote so a wiped product\n * doesn't leave orphan lots behind.\n */\nexport async function pruneLotsToBarcodes(barcodes: string[]): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  if (barcodes.length === 0) {\n    await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_lots`);\n    return;\n  }\n  const ids = barcodes.map((b) => `'${String(b).replace(/'/g, \"''\")}'`).join(',');\n  await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_lots WHERE barcode NOT IN (${ids})`);\n}\n"
}

256	export function translate(key: StringKey, lang: Lang): string {
257	  const entry = STR[key];

  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/components/BatchEditor.tsx",
  "content": "import { Calendar, Check, X } from 'lucide-react-native';\nimport React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { IconButton, QtyStepper, radius, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { useTheme } from '../../../theme';\nimport { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';\nimport { DatePickerModal } from './DatePickerModal';\n\nexport type Batch = {\n  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null = \"no expiry\". */\n  expiry: string | null;\n  qty: number;\n};\n\nfunction formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {\n  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');\n  return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;\n}\n\nfunction toISO(d: Date): string {\n  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];\n}\n\n/**\n * Edits a list of per-expiry batches for receiving / corrections. Each\n * row carries `{qty, expiry}`. Tapping the date opens the picker; the\n * checkbox toggles \"no expiry\" for that single row. Replaces the legacy\n * \"different expiry per pack\" toggle that exploded into N qty=1 rows\n * even when the guard had three groups, not ten packs.\n */\nexport function BatchEditor({\n  batches,\n  onChange,\n  minDate,\n  unit,\n}: {\n  batches: Batch[];\n  onChange: (next: Batch[]) => void;\n  minDate?: Date;\n  /** Optional unit string, e.g. \"g\" — appears next to total. */\n  unit?: string | null;\n}) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [editingIdx, setEditingIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const total = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0);\n\n  const update = (i: number, next: Partial<Batch>) => {\n    onChange(batches.map((b, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...b, ...next } : b)));\n  };\n  const remove = (i: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    onChange(batches.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));\n  };\n  const add = () => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    // Seed new batch with qty=1 and copy last expiry as a hint —\n    // common case is \"another batch on the same day\".\n    const last = batches[batches.length - 1];\n    onChange([...batches, { qty: 1, expiry: last?.expiry ?? null }]);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>\n      <View\n        style={{\n          flexDirection: 'row',\n          justifyContent: 'space-between',\n          alignItems: 'baseline',\n        }}\n      >\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {t('batches').toUpperCase()}\n        </Text>\n        <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {t('totalLabel')}: {total}\n          {unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n\n      {batches.map((b, i) => (\n        <View\n          key={i}\n          style={[\n            styles.row,\n            {\n              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n            },\n          ]}\n        >\n          <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: spacing.sm }}>\n            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>\n              <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ minWidth: 32 }}>\n                #{i + 1}\n              </Text>\n              <QtyStepper\n                value={b.qty}\n                onChange={(v) => update(i, { qty: v })}\n                min={1}\n              />\n            </View>\n\n            {b.expiry === null ? (\n              <Pressable\n                onPress={() => {\n                  haptic.tap();\n                  // Tapping \"no expiry\" row re-opens the picker so it's easy\n                  // to switch back. Seed with today.\n                  update(i, { expiry: toISO(new Date()) });\n                  setEditingIdx(i);\n                }}\n                style={[styles.dateRow, { borderColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}\n              >\n                <Calendar size={18} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />\n                <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm }}>\n                  {t('noExpiry')}\n                </Text>\n              </Pressable>\n            ) : (\n              <Pressable\n                onPress={() => setEditingIdx(i)}\n                style={[styles.dateRow, { borderColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}\n              >\n                <Calendar size={18} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />\n                <Text\n                  variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n                  color={palette.onSurface}\n                  style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, letterSpacing: 1 }}\n                >\n                  {formatForDisplay(b.expiry)}\n                </Text>\n              </Pressable>\n            )}\n\n            <Pressable\n              onPress={() => {\n                haptic.tap();\n                update(i, { expiry: b.expiry === null ? toISO(new Date()) : null });\n              }}\n              style={styles.noExpRow}\n            >\n              <View\n                style={[\n                  styles.checkbox,\n                  {\n                    backgroundColor: b.expiry === null ? palette.primary : 'transparent',\n                    borderColor: b.expiry === null ? palette.primary : palette.outline,\n                  },\n                ]}\n              >\n                {b.expiry === null && <Check size={12} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}\n              </View>\n              <Text variant=\"bodySmall\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n                {t('noExpiry')}\n              </Text>\n            </Pressable>\n          </View>\n\n          {batches.length > 1 && (\n            <IconButton\n              Icon={X}\n              onPress={() => remove(i)}\n              accessibilityLabel={t('removeBatch')}\n            />\n          )}\n        </View>\n      ))}\n\n      <Pressable\n        onPress={add}\n        style={[\n          styles.addBtn,\n          { borderColor: palette.outline, backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLow },\n        ]}\n      >\n        <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={palette.primary}>\n          {t('addBatch')}\n        </Text>\n      </Pressable>\n\n      {editingIdx != null && (\n        <DatePickerModal\n          visible={editingIdx != null}\n          value={batches[editingIdx]?.expiry ? new Date(batches[editingIdx].expiry as string) : null}\n          minimumDate={minDate}\n          onSelect={(d) => {\n            update(editingIdx, { expiry: toISO(d) });\n          }}\n          onDismiss={() => setEditingIdx(null)}\n        />\n      )}\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  row: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'flex-start',\n    padding: spacing.md,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1,\n    gap: spacing.sm,\n  },\n  dateRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,\n    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1,\n    minHeight: 44,\n  },\n  noExpRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    gap: spacing.sm,\n  },\n  checkbox: {\n    width: 18,\n    height: 18,\n    borderRadius: 4,\n    borderWidth: 2,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n  },\n  addBtn: {\n    paddingVertical: spacing.md,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1.5,\n    borderStyle: 'dashed',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n  },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/components/LotPicker.tsx",
  "content": "import React, { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { QtyStepper, radius, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { useTheme } from '../../../theme';\n\nexport type LotAvailability = {\n  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null = no expiry. */\n  expiry: string | null;\n  /** Total currently in stock for this lot. */\n  available: number;\n};\n\nexport type LotAllocation = LotAvailability & {\n  /** How much the guard is taking from this lot. 0 = none. */\n  take: number;\n};\n\nfunction formatForDisplay(iso: string | null): string {\n  if (iso === null) return '—';\n  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');\n  return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Suggest a FEFO allocation: take from the earliest-expiry lot first.\n * Returns one entry per available lot (zero `take` if not used). Lots\n * are expected in FEFO order from the caller.\n */\nexport function suggestFEFO(\n  lots: LotAvailability[],\n  totalQty: number,\n): LotAllocation[] {\n  let remaining = totalQty;\n  return lots.map((l) => {\n    const take = Math.max(0, Math.min(l.available, remaining));\n    remaining -= take;\n    return { ...l, take };\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Per-lot qty picker for dispense. Lots are listed in FEFO order with\n * the earliest highlighted as \"suggested\" — this is the **soft nudge**:\n * we record what the guard actually picked rather than enforcing FEFO,\n * so accounting matches physical reality. Guards can adjust each row;\n * the editor enforces the per-lot ceiling so they can't take more than\n * exists in any batch.\n *\n * The component is fully controlled — caller owns `lots` and the\n * resulting allocation via `onChange`. Total taken is derived from\n * `lots.reduce((s, l) => s + l.take, 0)` and displayed at the bottom.\n */\nexport function LotPicker({\n  lots,\n  onChange,\n  desiredQty,\n  unit,\n}: {\n  /** Allocation rows. Order = FEFO from caller. */\n  lots: LotAllocation[];\n  onChange: (next: LotAllocation[]) => void;\n  /** What the guard initially asked for. Used for the \"Taken N of M\" badge. */\n  desiredQty: number;\n  unit?: string | null;\n}) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n\n  const total = useMemo(() => lots.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.take || 0), 0), [lots]);\n\n  // Auto-seed allocation with FEFO when the parent passes the freshly-\n  // loaded availability without any takes set. Idempotent — caller\n  // controls reseeding by changing the `lots` reference.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const noneAllocated = lots.every((l) => l.take === 0);\n    if (noneAllocated && desiredQty > 0) {\n      onChange(suggestFEFO(lots, desiredQty));\n    }\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [lots.length]);\n\n  if (lots.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <View>\n        <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          —\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>\n      <View\n        style={{\n          flexDirection: 'row',\n          justifyContent: 'space-between',\n          alignItems: 'baseline',\n        }}\n      >\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {t('pickFromBatch').toUpperCase()}\n        </Text>\n        <Text\n          variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n          color={total === desiredQty ? palette.primary : palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n        >\n          {t('takenTotal')}: {total} {t('ofTotal')} {desiredQty}\n          {unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n\n      {lots.map((lot, i) => {\n        const isFefoSuggested = i === 0;\n        return (\n          <View\n            key={`${lot.expiry ?? '__none__'}-${i}`}\n            style={[\n              styles.row,\n              {\n                backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n                borderColor:\n                  isFefoSuggested && lot.take > 0\n                    ? palette.primary\n                    : palette.outlineVariant,\n                borderWidth: isFefoSuggested && lot.take > 0 ? 2 : 1,\n              },\n            ]}\n          >\n            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>\n                <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={palette.onSurface} style={{ letterSpacing: 1 }}>\n                  {formatForDisplay(lot.expiry)}\n                </Text>\n                {isFefoSuggested && (\n                  <Text variant=\"labelSmall\" color={palette.primary}>\n                    ← {t('suggested')}\n                  </Text>\n                )}\n              </View>\n              <Text variant=\"bodySmall\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>\n                {lot.available} {t('inStock')}\n              </Text>\n            </View>\n            <QtyStepper\n              value={lot.take}\n              onChange={(v) => {\n                const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(lot.available, v));\n                onChange(lots.map((l, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...l, take: clamped } : l)));\n              }}\n              min={0}\n              max={lot.available}\n            />\n          </View>\n        );\n      })}\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  row: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    padding: spacing.md,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    gap: spacing.md,\n  },\n});\n"
}

  "old_string": "function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {\n  try {\n    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');\n    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;\n  } catch {\n    return iso;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction toISO(d: Date): string {\n  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];\n}\n\n/** Earliest date out of a list; nulls ignored. Returns null if all null. */\nfunction earliestISO(dates: (Date | null)[]): string | null {\n  const isos = dates.filter((d): d is Date => !!d).map(toISO).sort();\n  return isos[0] ?? null;\n}",
  "new_string": "function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {\n  try {\n    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');\n    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;\n  } catch {\n    return iso;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Earliest non-null ISO date out of a list of batches. */\nfunction earliestExpiry(batches: Batch[]): string | null {\n  const sorted = batches\n    .map((b) => b.expiry)\n    .filter((e): e is string => !!e)\n    .sort();\n  return sorted[0] ?? null;\n}"
}
