75	export function EditStockScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
76	  const t = useT();
77	  const { palette } = useTheme();
78	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
79	  const { barcode } = route.params;
80	
81	  const [row, setRow] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);
82	  const [originalQty, setOriginalQty] = useState<number>(0);
83	  const [originalExpiry, setOriginalExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);
84	  const [qty, setQty] = useState<number>(0);
85	  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);
86	  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState<boolean>(false);
87	
88	  // Per-pack expiry. Mirrors ReceivingScreen: off by default; appears only
89	  // when qty > 1 and not noExpiry. When on, expand into N rows so the
90	  // guard can set a different expiry on each pack.
91	  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);
92	  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);
93	  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
94	
95	  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);
96	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
97	
98	  // JIT verification — fetch the server's authoritative on_hand +
99	  // nearest_expiry the moment the screen opens, in parallel with the
100	  // local read. If the server differs, we surface a "Stock has changed"
101	  // prompt before letting the user save. If the fetch fails (timeout /
102	  // offline / 5xx), we block the save entirely — corrections must be
103	  // committed against verified state per the cache-freshness plan.
104	  const [verifyStatus, setVerifyStatus] = useState<VerifyStatus>('verifying');
105	  const [serverQty, setServerQty] = useState<number | null>(null);
106	  const [serverExpiry, setServerExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);
107	
108	  useEffect(() => {
109	    let cancelled = false;
110	    (async () => {
111	      const r = await findStock(barcode);
112	      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);
113	      if (cancelled) return;
114	      if (!r) return;
115	      setRow(r);
116	      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);
117	      setOriginalExpiry(exp);
118	      setQty(r.on_hand);
119	      if (exp) {
120	        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));
121	        setNoExpiry(false);
122	      } else {
123	        setNoExpiry(true);
124	      }
125	    })();
126	    return () => { cancelled = true; };
127	  }, [barcode]);
128	
129	  // Fire the JIT fetch independently of the local read so neither
130	  // blocks the other. The 3 s timeout matches CatalogPickerScreen.
131	  useEffect(() => {
132	    let cancelled = false;
133	    const controller = new AbortController();
134	    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);
135	    (async () => {
136	      try {
137	        const remote = await api.fetch.stockOne(barcode);
138	        if (cancelled) return;
139	        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);
140	        const remoteExpiry = remote.nearest_expiry ?? null;
141	        setServerQty(remoteQty);
142	        setServerExpiry(remoteExpiry);
143	        setVerifyStatus('ok'); // narrowed to 'conflict' below once we know local values
144	      } catch (err) {
145	        if (cancelled) return;
146	        // 404 = no stock row on server; treat as authoritative "absent"
147	        // — the correction will create it. Same UX as a clean ok.
148	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
149	          setVerifyStatus('absent');
150	          return;
151	        }
152	        // 401 is handled centrally; clear navigates to Login.
153	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;
154	        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');
155	      } finally {
156	        clearTimeout(t);
157	      }
158	    })();
159	    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };
160	  }, [barcode]);
161	
162	  // Once both local and server have settled, classify ok-vs-conflict.
163	  // Conflict iff server differs from what the user is about to edit.
164	  // We use originalQty/originalExpiry as the "what the user opened
165	  // with" reference — those don't change unless the user explicitly
166	  // accepts the new server values.
167	  useEffect(() => {
168	    if (verifyStatus !== 'ok' || row === null || serverQty === null) return;
169	    const qtyDiffers = Math.abs(serverQty - originalQty) > 1e-9;
170	    const expiryDiffers = (serverExpiry ?? null) !== (originalExpiry ?? null);
171	    if (qtyDiffers || expiryDiffers) setVerifyStatus('conflict');
172	  }, [verifyStatus, row, serverQty, serverExpiry, originalQty, originalExpiry]);
173	
174	  // Keep the per-pack list in sync with qty when toggle is on. Seed new
175	  // rows with the bulk expiry so the guard only edits the differing ones.
176	  useEffect(() => {
177	    if (!perPackExpiry) return;
178	    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {
179	      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));
180	      if (prev.length === n) return prev;
181	      const next = [...prev];
182	      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);
183	      while (next.length > n) next.pop();
184	      return next;
185	    });
186	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);
187	
188	  // Auto-disable per-pack when the toggle's preconditions go away
189	  // (qty drops to 1, or guard checks "no expiry").
190	  useEffect(() => {
191	    if (perPackExpiry && (qty <= 1 || noExpiry)) {
192	      setPerPackExpiry(false);
193	    }
194	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, noExpiry]);
195	
196	  /** Single new_expiry sent to server when per-pack is off. */
197	  const newSingleExpiry = useMemo(() => {
198	    if (noExpiry || perPackExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;
199	    return toISO(expiryDateObj);
200	  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry, perPackExpiry]);
201	
202	  /** Array of expiries sent when per-pack is on. */
203	  const newExpiriesArray = useMemo<string[] | null>(() => {
204	    if (noExpiry || !perPackExpiry) return null;
205	    return perPackExpiries.map((d) => (d ? toISO(d) : '')).filter((s) => s.length > 0);
206	  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries]);
207	
208	  /** Whatever ends up as the locally-tracked "nearest" expiry. */
209	  const localNearestExpiry = useMemo(() => {
210	    if (noExpiry) return null;
211	    if (perPackExpiry) return earliestISO(perPackExpiries);
212	    return expiryDateObj ? toISO(expiryDateObj) : null;
213	  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries, expiryDateObj]);
214	
215	  const changed = useMemo(() => {
216	    if (!row) return false;
217	    if (qty !== originalQty) return true;
218	    if (localNearestExpiry !== originalExpiry) return true;
219	    // Per-pack with same nearest as original still counts as a change if
220	    // any pack carries a date different from the bulk default.
221	    if (perPackExpiry) return true;
222	    return false;
223	  }, [row, qty, originalQty, localNearestExpiry, originalExpiry, perPackExpiry]);
224	
225	  const packDisplay = row && row.pack_size != null && row.pack_size !== 1 && row.unit
226	    ? `${row.pack_size} ${row.unit}`
227	    : row?.unit ?? null;
228	
229	  // When pack_size > 1 the count is in packs, not in the base unit —
230	  // " 1 g" is wrong for a 200 g pack. Drop the unit suffix in that case
231	  // (the pack hint at the top already shows "200 g").
232	  const countSuffix = row && row.pack_size != null && row.pack_size !== 1
233	    ? ''
234	    : row?.unit
235	      ? ` ${row.unit}`
236	      : '';
237	
238	  /**
239	   * User accepted the server's view. Rebase originalQty / originalExpiry
240	   * and also replace what's in the steppers / date picker so the
241	   * "current count" line and the inputs match. The audit record's
242	   * old_qty / old_expiry will now reflect the actual transition.
243	   */
244	  const acceptServerView = () => {
245	    if (serverQty === null) return;
246	    haptic.tap();
247	    setOriginalQty(serverQty);
248	    setQty(serverQty);
249	    setOriginalExpiry(serverExpiry ?? null);
250	    if (serverExpiry) {
251	      setExpiryDateObj(new Date(serverExpiry));
252	      setNoExpiry(false);
253	    } else {
254	      setExpiryDateObj(null);
255	      setNoExpiry(true);
256	    }
257	    setPerPackExpiry(false);
258	    setPerPackExpiries([]);
259	    setVerifyStatus('ok');
260	  };
261	
262	  /**
263	   * User chose to keep their edit. We still rebase originalQty /
264	   * originalExpiry to the SERVER's truth so the audit record reflects
265	   * "server was N, guard set it to M" — not "guard thought it was N
266	   * (stale), set it to M". Steppers untouched.
267	   */
268	  const keepUserEdit = () => {
269	    if (serverQty === null) return;
270	    haptic.tap();
271	    setOriginalQty(serverQty);
272	    setOriginalExpiry(serverExpiry ?? null);
273	    setVerifyStatus('ok');
274	  };
275	
276	  const submit = async () => {
277	    if (!row || !changed) return;
278	    const session = getSession();
279	    setSaving(true);
280	    try {
281	      await correctStockLocal(barcode, qty, localNearestExpiry);
282	      await enqueue('stock_correction', {
283	        id: `crn_${nanoid(12)}`,
284	        barcode,
285	        product_name: row.name,
286	        old_qty: originalQty,
287	        new_qty: qty,
288	        old_expiry: originalExpiry,
289	        new_expiry: newSingleExpiry,
290	        new_expiries: newExpiriesArray,
291	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
292	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
293	        corrected_at: Date.now(),
294	      });
295	      // Await the flush so the StockScreen's focus refresh sees the
296	      // server-acknowledged value, not stale read-back data. Without
297	      // this, the GET races the POST and replaceStockFromRemote
298	      // overwrites the local optimistic write.
299	      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
300	      haptic.success();
301	      navigation.goBack();
302	    } finally {
303	      setSaving(false);
304	    }
305	  };
306	
307	  if (!row) {
308	    return (
309	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
310	        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
311	      </View>
312	    );
313	  }
314

  "old_string": "  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    (async () => {\n      const r = await findStock(barcode);\n      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);\n      if (cancelled) return;\n      if (!r) return;\n      setRow(r);\n      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);\n      setOriginalExpiry(exp);\n      setQty(r.on_hand);\n      if (exp) {\n        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));\n        setNoExpiry(false);\n      } else {\n        setNoExpiry(true);\n      }\n    })();\n    return () => { cancelled = true; };\n  }, [barcode]);",
  "new_string": "  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    (async () => {\n      const r = await findStock(barcode);\n      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);\n      const lots = await listLots(barcode);\n      if (cancelled) return;\n      if (!r) return;\n      setRow(r);\n      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);\n      setOriginalExpiry(exp);\n      // Seed batches from local lots. If lots are empty (legacy row from\n      // before the lots migration, or fresh barcode), synthesise one\n      // batch carrying the entire on_hand at the cached nearest_expiry.\n      const seed: Batch[] = lots.length > 0\n        ? lots.map((l) => ({ expiry: l.expiry_date, qty: Number(l.qty) }))\n        : [{ expiry: exp, qty: r.on_hand || 1 }];\n      setOriginalBatches(seed);\n      setBatches(seed);\n    })();\n    return () => { cancelled = true; };\n  }, [barcode]);"
}

  "old_string": "  // Keep the per-pack list in sync with qty when toggle is on. Seed new\n  // rows with the bulk expiry so the guard only edits the differing ones.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!perPackExpiry) return;\n    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {\n      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));\n      if (prev.length === n) return prev;\n      const next = [...prev];\n      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);\n      while (next.length > n) next.pop();\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);\n\n  // Auto-disable per-pack when the toggle's preconditions go away\n  // (qty drops to 1, or guard checks \"no expiry\").\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (perPackExpiry && (qty <= 1 || noExpiry)) {\n      setPerPackExpiry(false);\n    }\n  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, noExpiry]);\n\n  /** Single new_expiry sent to server when per-pack is off. */\n  const newSingleExpiry = useMemo(() => {\n    if (noExpiry || perPackExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;\n    return toISO(expiryDateObj);\n  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry, perPackExpiry]);\n\n  /** Array of expiries sent when per-pack is on. */\n  const newExpiriesArray = useMemo<string[] | null>(() => {\n    if (noExpiry || !perPackExpiry) return null;\n    return perPackExpiries.map((d) => (d ? toISO(d) : '')).filter((s) => s.length > 0);\n  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries]);\n\n  /** Whatever ends up as the locally-tracked \"nearest\" expiry. */\n  const localNearestExpiry = useMemo(() => {\n    if (noExpiry) return null;\n    if (perPackExpiry) return earliestISO(perPackExpiries);\n    return expiryDateObj ? toISO(expiryDateObj) : null;\n  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries, expiryDateObj]);\n\n  const changed = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!row) return false;\n    if (qty !== originalQty) return true;\n    if (localNearestExpiry !== originalExpiry) return true;\n    // Per-pack with same nearest as original still counts as a change if\n    // any pack carries a date different from the bulk default.\n    if (perPackExpiry) return true;\n    return false;\n  }, [row, qty, originalQty, localNearestExpiry, originalExpiry, perPackExpiry]);",
  "new_string": "  /** Total qty across all batches — replaces the old single qty stepper. */\n  const qty = useMemo(() => batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0), [batches]);\n\n  /** Whatever ends up as the locally-tracked \"nearest\" expiry. */\n  const localNearestExpiry = useMemo(() => earliestExpiry(batches), [batches]);\n\n  /** True iff batches differ from what was loaded — qty or per-batch\n   *  expiry. Counts re-orderings as no change. */\n  const changed = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!row) return false;\n    const a = [...batches]\n      .map((b) => `${b.expiry ?? ''}@${b.qty}`)\n      .sort()\n      .join('|');\n    const b = [...originalBatches]\n      .map((b) => `${b.expiry ?? ''}@${b.qty}`)\n      .sort()\n      .join('|');\n    return a !== b;\n  }, [row, batches, originalBatches]);"
}

Without\n      // this, the GET races the POST and replaceStockFromRemote\n      // overwrites the local optimistic write.\n      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };",

Server's correction\n        // route prefers `lots` over the legacy `new_expiry` /\n        // `new_expiries` fields.\n        lots: cleanBatches.map((b) => ({ expiry_date: b.expiry, qty: b.qty })),\n        // Legacy fields kept for backward compatibility with older\n        // server builds and dashboard audit views.\n        new_expiry: cleanBatches.length === 1 ? cleanBatches[0].expiry : localNearestExpiry,\n        new_expiries: null,\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n        corrected_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n      // Await the flush so the StockScreen's focus refresh sees the\n      // server-acknowledged value, not stale read-back data. Without\n      // this, the GET races the POST and replaceStockFromRemote\n      // overwrites the local optimistic write.\n      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };"
}

35	function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {
36	  try {
37	    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
38	    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
39	  } catch {
40	    return iso;
41	  }
42	}
43	
44	/** Earliest non-null ISO date out of a list of batches. */
45	function earliestExpiry(batches: Batch[]): string | null {
46	  const sorted = batches
47	    .map((b) => b.expiry)
48	    .filter((e): e is string => !!e)
49	    .sort();
50	  return sorted[0] ?? null;

  "old_string": "  const submit = async () => {\n    if (!selected) return;\n    const session = getSession();\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await enqueue('dispense', {\n        id: `dsp_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        barcode: selected.barcode,\n        product_name: selected.name,\n        qty,\n        reason,\n        // taken_by is now always the logged-in guard — no separate\n        // free-text \"who\" field. Removes one mandatory input from the\n        // dispense flow.\n        taken_by: session?.guardName ?? null,\n        issued_at: Date.now(),\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n      });\n      await adjustOnHand(selected.barcode, -qty);\n      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };",
  "new_string": "  const totalTaken = useMemo(\n    () => allocation.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.take || 0), 0),\n    [allocation],\n  );\n\n  const submit = async () => {\n    if (!selected || totalTaken <= 0) return;\n    const session = getSession();\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      const picks = allocation\n        .filter((l) => l.take > 0)\n        .map((l) => ({ expiry_date: l.expiry, qty: l.take }));\n      await enqueue('dispense', {\n        id: `dsp_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        barcode: selected.barcode,\n        product_name: selected.name,\n        qty: totalTaken,\n        // Soft-nudge model: server records what the guard actually\n        // picked rather than enforcing FEFO. Empty picks → server\n        // falls back to FEFO (the suggestion the user accepted).\n        lot_picks: picks,\n        reason,\n        // taken_by is now always the logged-in guard — no separate\n        // free-text \"who\" field. Removes one mandatory input from the\n        // dispense flow.\n        taken_by: session?.guardName ?? null,\n        issued_at: Date.now(),\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n      });\n      await decrementLotsLocal(\n        selected.barcode,\n        totalTaken,\n        picks.map((p) => ({ expiry_date: p.expiry_date, qty: p.qty })),\n      );\n      await adjustOnHand(selected.barcode, -totalTaken);\n      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };"
}

  "old_string": "  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);\n  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  // Expiry date state — driven by native date picker\n  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);\n  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState(false);\n  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);\n  // Per-pack expiry. Default OFF — most bulk scans share one batch /\n  // one expiry. When ON, expand a list of N rows so the guard can set\n  // a different expiry on each pack of the multi-quantity scan.\n  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);\n  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);\n  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  // Keep the per-pack list in lockstep with qty when the toggle is on.\n  // Initialise new rows with the bulk expiry so the guard can edit\n  // only the ones that actually differ.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!perPackExpiry) return;\n    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {\n      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));\n      if (prev.length === n) return prev;\n      const next = [...prev];\n      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);\n      while (next.length > n) next.pop();\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);",
  "new_string": "  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);\n  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  // One row per distinct expiry. Default to a single batch carrying\n  // qty=1 — matches the 90% case of \"one pack, one expiry\". Guard taps\n  // \"+ Add another expiry batch\" to break into groups.\n  const [batches, setBatches] = useState<Batch[]>([{ qty: 1, expiry: null }]);\n  const qty = useMemo(() => batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0), [batches]);"
}

  "old_string": "  /** Standalone receipt path — when no order session is active */\n  const persistStandalone = async (flagged: boolean) => {\n    const session = getSession();\n    // Per-pack expiry: split into N qty=1 receipts so each pack carries\n    // its own expiry date. Single-expiry path stays as one bulk row.\n    const receipts: Array<{ id: string; qty: number; expiry: string | null }> = perPackExpiry\n      ? perPackExpiries.map((d) => ({\n          id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`,\n          qty: 1,\n          expiry: d ? toISO(d) : null,\n        }))\n      : [{ id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`, qty, expiry: expiryDate }];\n\n    // The first receipt's id is what we use to link mismatch flags etc.\n    const id = receipts[0].id;\n\n    for (const r of receipts) {\n      await enqueue('receipt', {\n        id: r.id,\n        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,\n        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,\n        product_id: productId ?? null,\n        barcode,\n        product_name: resolvedName,\n        qty: r.qty,\n        expiry_date: r.expiry,\n        flagged,\n        scanned_at: Date.now(),\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n      });\n    }",
  "new_string": "  /** Standalone receipt path — when no order session is active */\n  const persistStandalone = async (flagged: boolean) => {\n    const session = getSession();\n    // One receipt per distinct expiry batch. Each batch carries its\n    // total qty (no exploding into N qty=1 rows).\n    const cleanBatches = batches.filter((b) => b.qty > 0);\n    const receipts: Array<{ id: string; qty: number; expiry: string | null }> =\n      cleanBatches.map((b) => ({\n        id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        qty: b.qty,\n        expiry: b.expiry,\n      }));\n\n    // The first receipt's id is what we use to link mismatch flags etc.\n    const id = receipts[0]?.id ?? `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;\n\n    for (const r of receipts) {\n      await enqueue('receipt', {\n        id: r.id,\n        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,\n        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,\n        product_id: productId ?? null,\n        barcode,\n        product_name: resolvedName,\n        qty: r.qty,\n        expiry_date: r.expiry,\n        flagged,\n        scanned_at: Date.now(),\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n      });\n      // Mirror the lot locally so EditStock / Dispense see the new\n      // batch before the next sync round-trip.\n      await addLotLocal(barcode, r.expiry, r.qty);\n    }"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/.claude/projects/-Users-appointy-work-OfficeOperationsUmbrella/memory/project_inventory_lots.md",
  "content": "---\nname: project-inventory-lots\ndescription: Stock is tracked as per-expiry lots (batches) end-to-end; dispense uses a soft-nudge FEFO suggestion rather than enforcement.\nmetadata:\n  type: project\n---\n\nInventory is tracked as **lots** (per-expiry batches): one row per (site, barcode, expiry) in `inventory_stock_lots` and the field-app's local `stock_lots`. `inventory_stock.on_hand` and `inventory_stock.nearest_expiry` are denormalized caches derived from lots via `refreshStockCache`. Receipts → `addLot`, corrections → `replaceLots`, dispenses → `decrementLots` (FEFO default, or explicit `lot_picks`).\n\n**Why:** Before this, the per-pack expiry toggle exploded \"10 packs in 3 groups\" into 10 individual rows. The dispense flow had zero expiry awareness, so accounting drifted from physical the moment one batch ran out before another. Admin dashboard's stock view couldn't see correction expiries because they only lived in the audit table.\n\n**Dispense stance — soft nudge, not enforcement:** the earliest-expiry lot gets a \"← suggested\" hint in `LotPicker` and FEFO is the default allocation, but the guard can adjust per-lot. Records what was actually picked rather than forcing policy compliance. The constraint here is staff literacy + physical reality (damaged packs, etc.), not FEFO discipline — a FEFO-violation pattern detector can come later if needed.\n\n**How to apply:** When touching inventory flows, write to lots first, let `refreshStockCache` update the denormalized columns. Read sides should prefer `lots[]` from the API; the cached `nearest_expiry` is a hint only. For the dashboard's `expiry_dates` column, the server now sends `\"3@DATE, 4@DATE\"` strings — render verbatim.\n\nRelated: [[feedback-bifrost-deploy-commit]] still applies — deploy with `--commit` because the schema migration in `postgres-schema.ts` runs on boot and you want the right image.\n"
}
```

All six tasks done. TypeScript clean across engine + field-app + dashboard.

## What changed

**Engine** (`office-ops-engine`)

- New `inventory_stock_lots` table (one row per `(site, barcode, expiry)`, unique on those keys). `inventory_stock.on_hand` and `nearest_expiry` are now caches kept in sync by `refreshStockCache`.
- `db.ts` helpers: `addLot`, `replaceLots`, `decrementLots` (FEFO default + optional explicit picks), `listLots`, `ensureStockRow`.
- `POST /api/inventory/receipts` → adds to lots instead of `adjustOnHand`.
- `POST /api/inventory/issues` → accepts `lot_picks: [{expiry_date, qty}]`; FEFO when absent. Returns the applied breakdown.
- `POST /api/inventory/corrections` → accepts `lots[]` (new), still understands legacy `new_expiry` / `new_expiries`. Defensive scaling if sum mismatches `new_qty`.
- Reads (`/stock`, `/stock/:barcode`) include `lots[]`; admin's `stock-snapshot` renders `expiry_dates` as `"3@DATE, 4@DATE"`.

**Field-app**

- Schema bump to v8; new `stock_lots` table mirroring server.
- `src/db/lots.ts` mirrors server helpers (`listLots`, `replaceLotsLocal`, `addLotLocal`, `decrementLotsLocal`, `pruneLotsToBarcodes`).
- New components: `BatchEditor` (Receiving + EditStock) and `LotPicker` (Dispense — "← suggested" hint on FEFO row, per-lot stepper bounded by lot stock).
- `EditStockScreen`, `DispenseScreen`, `ReceivingScreen` rewired; per-pack toggle and quick-qty chips removed. The 10-pack / 3-groups example becomes 3 rows.
- Sync layer updates lots in both periodic refetch and screen-level fetch; pruning keeps dropped barcodes consistent.

**Dashboard** (`ops-dashboard`)

No code change — the existing `expiry_dates` cell now receives the per-batch string from the server.

## Things to verify on a real device (I can't from here)

- Receive 10 packs as 3+4+3 → check dashboard shows `"3@DATE, 4@DATE, 3@DATE"`.
- Dispense 3 packs, override the FEFO suggestion to 1+1+1 → check each lot decremented correctly.
- EditStock conflict banner still triggers when remote on_hand drifts.
- Legacy stock rows with no lots yet → reads still populate from the receipts aggregate fallback until the first lot write.

Memory saved at `project_inventory_lots.md`.

18	/**
19	 * Big stepper for low-literacy users. Plus/minus circles flank a tappable
20	 * display: tapping the number swaps it for an inline TextInput so power
21	 * users can type the qty directly. Commits on blur or submit.
22	 */
23	export function QtyStepper({ value, onChange, min = 1, max, decimal = true }: Props) {
24	  const { palette } = useTheme();
25	  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
26	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(value));
27	  const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
28	
29	  useEffect(() => {
30	    if (!editing) setDraft(String(value));
31	  }, [value, editing]);
32	
33	  const clamp = (n: number) => {
34	    let out = n;
35	    if (Number.isNaN(out)) out = min;
36	    if (out < min) out = min;
37	    if (max !== undefined && out > max) out = max;
38	    return out;
39	  };
40	
41	  const bump = (delta: number) => {
42	    haptic.tap();
43	    onChange(clamp(value + delta));
44	  };
45	
46	  const startEdit = () => {
47	    haptic.tap();
48	    setDraft(String(value));
49	    setEditing(true);
50	    requestAnimationFrame(() => inputRef.current?.focus());
51	  };
52	
53	  /**
54	   * Propagate every keystroke to the parent so a submit handler that
55	   * fires before our `onBlur` (e.g. tapping a Confirm button) still sees
56	   * the latest qty. The local `draft` keeps the literal text so partial
57	   * states like "" don't snap the display while typing.
58	   */
59	  const parseValue = (text: string) => decimal ? parseFloat(text) : parseInt(text, 10);
60	  const validText = (text: string) =>
61	    decimal ? /^-?\d*\.?\d*$/.test(text) : /^-?\d*$/.test(text);
62	
63	  const onTextChange = (text: string) => {
64	    if (!validText(text)) return; // reject stray characters
65	    setDraft(text);
66	    if (text.trim() === '' || text === '.' || text === '-') {
67	      // Don't push min on every backspace; wait for commit.
68	      return;
69	    }
70	    const parsed = parseValue(text);
71	    if (!Number.isNaN(parsed)) onChange(clamp(parsed));
72	  };
73	
74	  const commit = () => {
75	    const next = clamp(parseValue(draft));
76	    onChange(next);
77	    setDraft(String(next));
78	    setEditing(false);
79	  };
80	
81	  return (
82	    <View style={styles.row}>
83	      <StepperButton Icon={Minus} onPress={() => bump(-1)} bg={palette.primary} fg={palette.onPrimary} ripple={palette.outlineVariant} />
84	      {editing ? (
85	        <TextInput
86	          ref={inputRef}
87	          value={draft}
88	          onChangeText={onTextChange}
89	          onBlur={commit}
90	          onSubmitEditing={commit}
91	          keyboardType={decimal ? "decimal-pad" : "number-pad"}
92	          selectTextOnFocus
93	          returnKeyType="done"
94	          style={[
95	            typo.displayLarge,
96	            styles.qtyText,
97	            { color: palette.onSurface, borderBottomColor: palette.primary },
98	          ]}
99	        />
100	      ) : (
101	        <Pressable
102	          onPress={startEdit}
103	          android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true }}
104	          hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
105	        >
106	          <Text variant="displayLarge" style={styles.qtyText}>
107	            {value}
108	          </Text>
109	        </Pressable>
110	      )}
111	      <StepperButton Icon={Plus} onPress={() => bump(1)} bg={palette.primary} fg={palette.onPrimary} ripple={palette.outlineVariant} />
112	    </View>
113	  );
114	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/CompactStepper.tsx",
  "content": "import { Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react-native';\nimport React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { radius, spacing } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = {\n  value: number;\n  onChange: (n: number) => void;\n  min?: number;\n  max?: number;\n};\n\n/**\n * Row-sized qty stepper. Same +/- semantics as QtyStepper but built for\n * list rows in BatchEditor and LotPicker — 36 px buttons, fixed width\n * value cell, no `displayLarge` typography. Use the big QtyStepper for\n * hero \"how many?\" inputs; this one for \"edit row N of M\".\n */\nexport function CompactStepper({ value, onChange, min = 0, max }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);\n  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(value));\n  const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!editing) setDraft(String(value));\n  }, [value, editing]);\n\n  const clamp = (n: number) => {\n    let out = n;\n    if (Number.isNaN(out)) out = min;\n    if (out < min) out = min;\n    if (max !== undefined && out > max) out = max;\n    return out;\n  };\n\n  const bump = (delta: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    onChange(clamp(value + delta));\n  };\n\n  const startEdit = () => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    setDraft(String(value));\n    setEditing(true);\n    requestAnimationFrame(() => inputRef.current?.focus());\n  };\n\n  const commit = () => {\n    const next = clamp(parseInt(draft, 10));\n    onChange(next);\n    setDraft(String(next));\n    setEditing(false);\n  };\n\n  const atMin = value <= min;\n  const atMax = max !== undefined && value >= max;\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.row}>\n      <Pressable\n        onPress={() => !atMin && bump(-1)}\n        android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: 22 }}\n        style={[\n          styles.btn,\n          {\n            backgroundColor: atMin ? palette.surfaceContainerLow : palette.primary,\n            opacity: atMin ? 0.5 : 1,\n          },\n        ]}\n      >\n        <Minus size={20} color={atMin ? palette.onSurfaceVariant : palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />\n      </Pressable>\n\n      {editing ? (\n        <TextInput\n          ref={inputRef}\n          value={draft}\n          onChangeText={(t) => {\n            if (!/^-?\\d*$/.test(t)) return;\n            setDraft(t);\n            if (t.trim() === '' || t === '-') return;\n            const parsed = parseInt(t, 10);\n            if (!Number.isNaN(parsed)) onChange(clamp(parsed));\n          }}\n          onBlur={commit}\n          onSubmitEditing={commit}\n          keyboardType=\"number-pad\"\n          selectTextOnFocus\n          returnKeyType=\"done\"\n          style={[styles.valueText, { color: palette.onSurface, borderBottomColor: palette.primary }]}\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <Pressable onPress={startEdit} hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}>\n          <Text variant=\"titleLarge\" style={styles.valueText} color={palette.onSurface}>\n            {value}\n          </Text>\n        </Pressable>\n      )}\n\n      <Pressable\n        onPress={() => !atMax && bump(1)}\n        android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: 22 }}\n        style={[\n          styles.btn,\n          {\n            backgroundColor: atMax ? palette.surfaceContainerLow : palette.primary,\n            opacity: atMax ? 0.5 : 1,\n          },\n        ]}\n      >\n        <Plus size={20} color={atMax ? palette.onSurfaceVariant : palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />\n      </Pressable>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  row: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    gap: spacing.md,\n  },\n  btn: {\n    width: 36,\n    height: 36,\n    borderRadius: radius.pill,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    overflow: 'hidden',\n  },\n  valueText: {\n    minWidth: 36,\n    textAlign: 'center',\n    paddingBottom: 2,\n    borderBottomWidth: 1,\n    borderBottomColor: 'transparent',\n  },\n});\n"
}

80	export function DispenseScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
81	  const t = useT();
82	  const { palette } = useTheme();
83	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
84	  const initialBarcode = route.params?.barcode;
85	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);
86	  const [all, setAll] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);
87	  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
88	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
89	  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All');
90	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
91	  const [reason, setReason] = useState(REASONS[0]);
92	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
93	  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);
94	
95	  // JIT verification — fetch the server's authoritative on_hand the
96	  // moment a product is selected. Dispense MUST commit against the
97	  // freshest count so we never let a guard dispense 10 from a stock
98	  // of 8. Fail-closed: if the JIT fetch fails or times out, the
99	  // confirm button stays disabled until the user goes back and tries
100	  // again with network.
101	  const [verifyStatus, setVerifyStatus] = useState<VerifyStatus>('verifying');
102	  const [verifiedOnHand, setVerifiedOnHand] = useState<number | null>(null);
103	  /** Per-lot availability + current allocation. FEFO-ordered (earliest
104	   *  first). Source of truth for what the guard is dispensing — replaces
105	   *  the single `qty` stepper. Total taken = sum(allocation.take). */
106	  const [allocation, setAllocation] = useState<LotAllocation[]>([]);
107	
108	  const categoryOptions = useMemo<FilterOption[]>(() => {
109	    const cats = new Set(all.map((r) => r.category).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
110	    return [{ key: 'All', label: 'All Categories' }, ...Array.from(cats).sort().map((c) => ({ key: c, label: c }))];
111	  }, [all]);
112	
113	  useEffect(() => {
114	    listStock().then((rows) => {
115	      setAll(rows);
116	      setInitialLoading(false);
117	    });
118	  }, []);
119	
120	  useEffect(() => {
121	    if (initialBarcode) {
122	      findStock(initialBarcode).then((r) => r && setSelected(r));
123	    }
124	  }, [initialBarcode]);
125	
126	  // Whenever an item gets selected, re-run JIT verification. The
127	  // dependency on `selected?.barcode` (not the whole row) means
128	  // refetching only happens for an actual product change, not for the
129	  // optimistic update we apply after submit.
130	  useEffect(() => {
131	    if (!selected) {
132	      setVerifyStatus('verifying');
133	      setVerifiedOnHand(null);
134	      return;
135	    }
136	    let cancelled = false;
137	    setVerifyStatus('verifying');
138	    setVerifiedOnHand(null);
139	    const controller = new AbortController();
140	    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);
141	    (async () => {
142	      try {
143	        const remote = await api.fetch.stockOne(selected.barcode);
144	        if (cancelled) return;
145	        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);
146	        setVerifiedOnHand(remoteQty);
147	        setVerifyStatus('ok');
148	        // Clamp qty to the verified ceiling. If the user had a stale
149	        // higher qty in mind (e.g. local cache said 10, server says 7),
150	        // drop to the new max so they can't accidentally submit over.
151	        const clampedQty = qty > remoteQty ? Math.max(1, remoteQty) : qty;
152	        if (clampedQty !== qty) setQty(clampedQty);
153	
154	        // Build the lot picker state. Prefer the remote lots (verified
155	        // truth); fall back to the local lots cache when the server
156	        // returned no breakdown (legacy rows pre-lots migration).
157	        const remoteLots = remote.lots ?? [];
158	        const lots = remoteLots.length > 0
159	          ? remoteLots
160	          : (await listLots(selected.barcode)).map((l) => ({
161	              expiry_date: l.expiry_date,
162	              qty: Number(l.qty),
163	            }));
164	        const availability = lots.map((l) => ({
165	          expiry: l.expiry_date,
166	          available: Number(l.qty),
167	        }));
168	        setAllocation(suggestFEFO(availability, clampedQty));
169	      } catch (err) {
170	        if (cancelled) return;
171	        // 404: the server has no record of this barcode (might be a
172	        // device-only item never received server-side). Treat as
173	        // "absent" — block dispense; you can't dispense what the
174	        // server doesn't know exists.
175	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
176	          setVerifyStatus('absent');
177	          return;
178	        }
179	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;
180	        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');
181	      } finally {
182	        clearTimeout(t);
183	      }
184	    })();
185	    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };
186	    // intentionally NOT in deps: `qty` would re-run the JIT on every
187	    // stepper tap; only refire when the selected barcode changes.
188	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
189	  }, [selected?.barcode]);
190	
191	  const results = useMemo(() => {
192	    if (selected) return [];
193	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
194	    return all
195	      .filter((r) => {
196	        if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;
197	        if (statusFilter !== 'All') {
198	          const s = statusFor(r);
199	          if (statusFilter === 'In Stock' && s !== 'ok') return false;
200	          if (statusFilter === 'Low' && s !== 'low') return false;
201	          if (statusFilter === 'Out' && s !== 'out') return false;
202	        }
203	        if (!q) return true;
204	        return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q) || (r.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
205	      })
206	      .slice(0, 30);
207	  }, [all, query, selected, category, statusFilter]);
208	
209	  const totalTaken = useMemo(
210	    () => allocation.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.take || 0), 0),
211	    [allocation],
212	  );
213	
214	  const submit = async () => {
215	    if (!selected || totalTaken <= 0) return;
216	    const session = getSession();
217	    setSaving(true);
218	    try {
219	      const picks = allocation
220	        .filter((l) => l.take > 0)
221	        .map((l) => ({ expiry_date: l.expiry, qty: l.take }));
222	      await enqueue('dispense', {
223	        id: `dsp_${nanoid(12)}`,
224	        barcode: selected.barcode,
225	        product_name: selected.name,
226	        qty: totalTaken,
227	        // Soft-nudge model: server records what the guard actually
228	        // picked rather than enforcing FEFO. Empty picks → server
229	        // falls back to FEFO (the suggestion the user accepted).
230	        lot_picks: picks,
231	        reason,
232	        // taken_by is now always the logged-in guard — no separate
233	        // free-text "who" field. Removes one mandatory input from the
234	        // dispense flow.
235	        taken_by: session?.guardName ?? null,
236	        issued_at: Date.now(),
237	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
238	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
239	      });
240	      await decrementLotsLocal(
241	        selected.barcode,
242	        totalTaken,
243	        picks.map((p) => ({ expiry_date: p.expiry_date, qty: p.qty })),
244	      );
245	      await adjustOnHand(selected.barcode, -totalTaken);
246	      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
247	      haptic.success();
248	      navigation.goBack();
249	    } finally {
250	      setSaving(false);
251	    }
252	  };
253	
254	  if (!selected) {
255	    return (
256	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
257	        <AppBar title={t('dispense')} subtitle={t('dispenseSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
258	        <View style={styles.search}>
259	          <TextInput
260	            value={query}
261	            onChangeText={setQuery}
262	            placeholder={t('search')}
263	            placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
264	            style={[
265	            styles.searchInput,
266	            {
267	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
268	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
269	              color: palette.onSurface,
270	            },
271	          ]}
272	            autoFocus
273	          />
274	        </View>

> AGENT

Pushed three fixes:

**1. The "broken UI" — wrong stepper size.** `BatchEditor` and `LotPicker` were using the hero 88px `QtyStepper` (designed for centered "HOW MANY?" prompts) inside small list rows — that's what broke the layout. New `CompactStepper` (36px) for in-row use; the big stepper stays where it belongs.

**2. The missing count UI on Dispense.** I'd dropped the big stepper entirely and tried to make per-lot pickers the only input. For 1-lot products there was nothing to drive `qty`, so the screen showed almost nothing. Now:

- Big "HOW MANY?" stepper is back as the primary input (with `max = total available across lots`).
- LotPicker only appears for 2+ lots — single-lot products keep the familiar one-stepper UX.
- When you bump the total stepper, FEFO re-allocates automatically across lots; bump per-lot in the picker only when you need to override.

**3. Legacy stock with no lots yet.** Existing inventory rows have no lots until something writes one. Dispense now synthesises a fallback lot from `on_hand` + `nearest_expiry` when the server returns an empty lots array, so dispense works on pre-migration stock.

## What to do on the device

**No native rebuild needed.** JS/TS only + a SQLite migration that runs at app launch. Just:

```bash
# in the field-app/ terminal, restart Metro:
npm start
# then on the phone: shake → "Reload"  (or  rr  in Metro)
```

If the app misbehaves around stock counts, force-quit and reopen — the SQLite migration to v8 (new `stock_lots` table) runs at db init.

TypeScript clean across all three packages. Worth doing one more round on the device on:
- Receive (BatchEditor row layout with the new compact stepper)
- Edit Stock (same)
- Dispense single-lot (big stepper only)
- Dispense multi-lot (big stepper + picker, override behaviour)

411	export async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {
412	  const db = await getDb();
413	  const result = await db.execute({
414	    sql: `
415	      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT}
416	      ${STOCK_JOINS}
417	      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.on_hand <= s.threshold
418	      ORDER BY s.on_hand ASC, s.name ASC
419	    `,
420	    args: [siteId],
421	  });

423	}

426	  siteId: string,
427	  barcode: string,
428	): Promise<InventoryStockRow | null> {
429	  const db = await getDb();

433	  });

435	}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT}\n      ${STOCK_JOINS}\n      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.on_hand <= s.threshold\n      ORDER BY s.on_hand ASC, s.name ASC\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockListRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},\n        to_char(s.nearest_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS nearest_expiry,\n        COALESCE(\n          (SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(\n                    'expiry_date', to_char(l.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),\n                    'qty', l.qty)\n                  ORDER BY l.expiry_date NULLS LAST)\n             FROM inventory_stock_lots l\n            WHERE l.site_id = s.site_id AND l.barcode = s.barcode AND l.qty > 0),\n          '[]'::json\n        ) AS lots\n      ${STOCK_JOINS}\n      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.on_hand <= s.threshold\n      ORDER BY s.on_hand ASC, s.name ASC\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows.map((r) => {\n    const row = r as Record<string, unknown>;\n    let lots = row.lots as LotSummary[] | string | null;\n    if (typeof lots === \"string\") {\n      try { lots = JSON.parse(lots) as LotSummary[]; } catch { lots = []; }\n    }\n    if (!Array.isArray(lots)) lots = [];\n    return {\n      ...(row as InventoryStockRow),\n      nearest_expiry: (row.nearest_expiry as string | null) ?? null,\n      lots: lots.map((l) => ({\n        expiry_date: l.expiry_date ?? null,\n        qty: Number(l.qty),\n      })),\n    } as InventoryStockListRow;\n  });\n}"
}

> AGENT

Done. Both tightened.

**OrderSession `submitAll`** — each receipted batch now calls `addLotLocal` immediately, so the moment a multi-item delivery wraps, EditStock and Dispense see the new lots without waiting for the next sync round-trip.

**Alerts endpoint** — `listLowOrOut` now mirrors `listStock`'s lot enrichment (json_agg in a single query), so AlertsScreen receives `lots[]` + `nearest_expiry` in the same shape as the stock list. No N+1.

TypeScript clean on both packages.

## What's still open (smaller things, your call)

- **AlertsScreen UX** — now has lots/nearest_expiry in the payload but doesn't yet surface "expiring soon" or per-batch breakdown. Easy to add when you want.
- **Dashboard correction history** — still renders the legacy `new_expiry` / `new_expiries` fields; doesn't yet show the `lots` payload from corrections. Useful for auditing what batches the guard set.
- **EditStock conflict accept** — when guard taps "Use new count" on the conflict banner, we collapse the server's breakdown into a single batch. Could fetch full server lots and preserve them.

None are blocking. Ready for device QA / commit whenever you want — I haven't committed anything per the repo rules. Just say the word.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/Planning-docs/inventory_lot_tracking_handoff.md",
  "content": "# Inventory Lot Tracking — Handoff\n\n**Status:** Implemented end-to-end, working on device. Not committed yet (per repo rule: no commits without explicit user instruction).\n\n**Scope:** Replaces per-pack expiry tracking with a per-expiry **lot** (batch) model across the engine, field-app, and dashboard. Adds soft-nudge FEFO dispense.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Context (read first)\n\nThe system has three relevant surfaces:\n\n- `office-ops-engine/` — Bun + Postgres backend, plugin-shaped. Inventory plugin lives in `src/mastra/modules/inventory/`. Schema in `src/mastra/lib/postgres-schema.ts`.\n- `field-app/` — Expo / React Native, offline-first SQLite. Inventory screens under `src/modules/inventory/screens/`. Local DB in `src/db/`. Outbox-driven sync via `src/sync/`.\n- `ops-dashboard/` — Vite + React 19 admin UI. Inventory module under `src/modules/inventory/`.\n\nThe umbrella `CLAUDE.md` documents the four operating constraints (cost, literacy, device capability, infrastructure). These shaped the design.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Why this exists\n\n**Problem 1 (stock list lag):** EditStock saves were racing the StockScreen refetch — `flushOnce` was fired-and-forgotten, so the GET hit the server before the POST. Local optimistic write got overwritten by stale server data.\n\n**Problem 2 (\"1g for a 200g pack\"):** EditStock's count line concatenated `${on_hand} ${unit}` with no awareness of pack_size. `on_hand=1` with `pack_size=200, unit=g` rendered as \"1 g\" instead of \"1 pack\".\n\n**Problem 3 (expiry invisible to admin):** Correction payloads wrote `new_expiry` only into the audit table `inventory_stock_corrections`. The admin Stock view's `expiry_dates` column was derived from `inventory_receipts.expiry_date` aggregate — guard corrections never reached it.\n\n**Problem 4 (the real one — multi-batch UX):** Per-pack expiry exploded \"10 packs in 3 expiry groups\" into 10 individual rows. And dispense had **no** expiry awareness at all — guards picked physical packs in any order while accounting silently assumed FIFO. The two would drift the moment one batch ran out.\n\nThe fix had to be a data-model change, not a UI tweak. Per-expiry lots is the standard inventory pattern; everything else flows from it.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Architecture decisions\n\n### Lot model\n\n- **Lots are the source of truth for on_hand.** `inventory_stock.on_hand` and `inventory_stock.nearest_expiry` are denormalized caches recomputed from lots via `refreshStockCache()` on every lot mutation.\n- **One lot row per `(site, barcode, expiry_date)`.** Receipts with the same expiry merge into the existing row. Unique index enforces this — the NULL-expiry case is handled with a sentinel (`DATE '0001-01-01'` in Postgres, `'__no_expiry__'` in SQLite).\n- **Receipts still get audit rows** in `inventory_receipts`. Lot writes are additional, not a replacement.\n- **Corrections wholesale-replace** lots for a barcode. The audit row in `inventory_stock_corrections` keeps the history.\n- **Dispenses decrement** lots either FEFO (default) or by explicit `lot_picks`.\n\n### Soft-nudge FEFO (the philosophical decision)\n\nWhen dispensing, the field-app **suggests** the FEFO lot (\"← suggested\" hint) but does not enforce it. Guards can override per-lot. The system records what was actually picked.\n\n**Rationale:** the constraint here is staff literacy + physical reality (damaged packs, mixed batches on shelves), not policy compliance. Enforcing FEFO would cause accounting to drift from physical when guards routed around it. A FEFO-violation alert can come later if pattern analysis warrants it.\n\n### Why not collapse `inventory_receipts` into lots?\n\nReceipts are an immutable audit history (who scanned what, when, against which order). Lots are mutable on-hand state. They serve different purposes; merging them would lose audit fidelity.\n\n---\n\n## 4. File-by-file changes\n\n### Engine (`office-ops-engine/`)\n\n| File | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `src/mastra/lib/postgres-schema.ts` | Added `inventory_stock_lots` table + `ALTER TABLE inventory_stock ADD COLUMN nearest_expiry DATE`. Both idempotent via `IF NOT EXISTS`. Unique index uses `COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01')` for NULL-expiry uniqueness. |\n| `src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts` | New helpers: `ensureStockRow`, `addLot`, `replaceLots`, `decrementLots`, `listLots`, `refreshStockCache` (internal). Updated `findStockJit` and `listStock` to return `lots[]` via `json_agg` subquery (single round-trip, no N+1). Same enrichment added to `listLowOrOut`. Updated `listStockForSheet` to render `expiry_dates` as `\"3@DATE, 4@DATE\"` from lots with fallback to receipt aggregate. |\n| `src/mastra/modules/inventory/routes.ts` | `POST /receipts` calls `ensureStockRow` + `addLot` instead of `adjustOnHand`. `POST /issues` accepts optional `lot_picks: [{expiry_date, qty}]`; returns applied picks. `POST /corrections` accepts `lots: [{expiry_date, qty}]` (preferred) and still understands legacy `new_expiry`/`new_expiries`. Removed unused `adjustOnHand`/`setStockOnHand` imports. |\n\n### Field-app (`field-app/`)\n\n| File | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `src/db/database.ts` | Schema version bumped to **8**. Migration adds local `stock_lots` table with same shape as server. Unique index uses sentinel `'__no_expiry__'`. |\n| `src/db/lots.ts` | **New file.** Local helpers mirroring server semantics: `listLots`, `replaceLotsLocal`, `addLotLocal`, `decrementLotsLocal`, `pruneLotsToBarcodes`. |\n| `src/sync/api.ts` | `RemoteStockRow` now has `lots?: RemoteLot[]` and `nearest_expiry?: string \\| null`. |\n| `src/sync/syncService.ts` | `refreshOne('stock')` and `refreshOne('alerts')` now mirror remote `lots[]` into local `stock_lots` via `replaceLotsLocal` per row, then `pruneLotsToBarcodes` to drop orphans. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/screens/StockScreen.tsx` | `fetchRemote` does the same lot mirroring (its independent refetch path). |\n| `src/design/CompactStepper.tsx` | **New file.** 36px row-sized +/- stepper. Sibling of the big 88px `QtyStepper` (hero \"How many?\" prompts) — using the big one inside list rows was what broke the UI in the first place. |\n| `src/design/index.ts` | Exports `CompactStepper`. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/components/BatchEditor.tsx` | **New file.** List of `{qty, expiry}` rows with \"+ Add another expiry batch\". Per-row \"no expiry\" checkbox. Used by Receiving and EditStock. Uses `CompactStepper`. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/components/LotPicker.tsx` | **New file.** FEFO-ordered list of lots with per-lot stepper bounded by lot stock. Earliest lot tagged \"← suggested\". Exports `suggestFEFO` helper. Used by Dispense. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/screens/EditStockScreen.tsx` | Per-pack toggle / single-expiry UI replaced with `BatchEditor`. Seeds batches from `listLots` on open; falls back to `[{on_hand, nearest_expiry}]` for legacy rows. Commits with `lots[]` payload + `replaceLotsLocal`. Awaits `flushOnce()` before goBack to close the StockScreen race. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/screens/DispenseScreen.tsx` | Brought back the big QtyStepper as the primary \"How many?\" input. `LotPicker` only renders when 2+ lots exist (single-lot case keeps the familiar one-stepper UX). Effect re-runs `suggestFEFO` when qty changes. Submit sends `lot_picks[]` and decrements local lots via `decrementLotsLocal`. **Legacy fallback:** if server returns no lots but `on_hand > 0`, synthesises one lot from `(on_hand, nearest_expiry)` so dispense works on pre-migration stock. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/screens/ReceivingScreen.tsx` | Per-pack toggle and QuickChip helpers removed. Replaced with `BatchEditor`. Each batch becomes one outbox receipt (not exploded into qty=1 rows). Mirrors lot via `addLotLocal`. |\n| `src/modules/inventory/components/OrderSessionContext.tsx` | `submitAll` now calls `addLotLocal` per batch so multi-item delivery sessions immediately reflect new lots in EditStock without waiting for sync. |\n| `src/i18n/strings.ts` | New keys: `batches`, `addBatch`, `removeBatch`, `totalLabel`, `pickFromBatch`, `suggested`, `inStock`, `takenTotal`, `ofTotal`. Hindi + English. |\n\n### Dashboard (`ops-dashboard/`)\n\n**No code changes.** The Stock view's `expiry_dates` cell renders the server-formatted `\"3@DATE, 4@DATE\"` string verbatim. Correction history still renders the legacy `new_expiry`/`new_expiries` fields (see Open Items).\n\n---\n\n## 5. API contract changes\n\n### `POST /api/inventory/receipts`\n\nUnchanged payload. Server side now also creates/merges a lot keyed on `(site, barcode, expiry_date)`.\n\n### `POST /api/inventory/issues`\n\n**Added optional field:** `lot_picks: [{expiry_date: string|null, qty: number}]`.\n\nWhen omitted → server decrements FEFO. When present → server decrements those specific lots (clamped per-lot).\n\n**Response now includes:** `lot_picks: [{expiry_date, qty}]` — the actual applied breakdown (may differ from requested if a lot was short).\n\n### `POST /api/inventory/corrections`\n\n**Added preferred field:** `lots: [{expiry_date: string|null, qty: number}]`.\n\nServer precedence: `lots` → legacy `new_expiries` (collapses identical dates into a single lot) → legacy `new_expiry` (single lot with `new_qty`).\n\nDefensive: server clamps lot qtys to match `new_qty` if the array sums to a different total (proportional scale). Audit row still records original payload.\n\n### `GET /api/inventory/stock` & `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode` & `GET /api/inventory/alerts`\n\n**Added per-row:** `lots: [{expiry_date, qty}]` (FEFO-ordered, only `qty > 0`) and `nearest_expiry: string | null`.\n\n### `GET /api/inventory/stock-snapshot` (admin)\n\n`expiry_dates` field now formatted as `\"3@2026-08-15, 4@2026-09-10\"` from lots, fallback to legacy date aggregate for rows with no lots yet.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Migration & compatibility\n\n**Engine schema:** runs at boot from `ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema()`. `ALTER TABLE IF EXISTS` and `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` make it idempotent.\n\n**Lazy data migration:** existing `inventory_stock` rows have no `inventory_stock_lots` entries until a receipt or correction creates one. Read paths handle this:\n\n- `listStockForSheet` (admin): falls back to receipts aggregate.\n- `findStockJit` (field-app verify): falls back to MIN(receipts.expiry_date).\n- `DispenseScreen` (field-app): synthesises a single lot from `(on_hand, nearest_expiry)` so dispense isn't blocked on legacy stock.\n\n**Field-app schema v8:** runs via `runMigrations` in `database.ts`. Forward-only.\n\n**Backward compatibility:**\n- Old field-app builds sending only `new_expiry`/`new_expiries` to corrections still work (legacy paths preserved server-side).\n- Old issue payloads without `lot_picks` work (server FEFO default).\n- Old dashboard reads work (existing `expiry_dates` field shape preserved, just string contents richer).\n\n---\n\n## 7. Open items (priority order)\n\n### High\n- **AlertsScreen surface lots.** Engine payload now includes `lots[]` + `nearest_expiry` for alerts, but `src/modules/inventory/screens/AlertsScreen.tsx` doesn't render them. Easy win — show nearest expiry date in red when ≤ 30 days; show per-batch breakdown on tap.\n\n### Medium\n- **Dashboard correction history.** `ops-dashboard/src/modules/inventory/InventorySection.tsx:1898-1899` still renders `r.new_expiries ?? fmtDate(r.new_expiry)`. The server's `inventory_stock_corrections` table doesn't store the per-batch lots — only `new_qty`, `new_expiry`, `new_expiries[]`. To show full breakdown, either: (a) add a `lots` column to the audit table and persist it on correction, or (b) derive from `inventory_stock_lots` snapshot at correction time. (a) is cleaner.\n\n### Low\n- **EditStock conflict-accept preserves breakdown.** When guard taps \"Use new count\" on the conflict banner (`EditStockScreen.tsx:acceptServerView`), we collapse the server's lots into a single batch. Better: fetch the server's full lot breakdown and seed `batches` from it. The `findStockJit` response already includes `lots[]` — just plumb it through.\n- **`expiry_tracking` table cleanup.** Field-app's `expiry_tracking` (migration v2) is now partially redundant with `stock_lots`. Used by some alert flows. Decide whether to deprecate or keep as a per-receipt audit.\n\n### Nice-to-have (not urgent)\n- **FEFO-violation pattern detector.** With dispense `lot_picks` recorded, you can detect patterns where guards repeatedly skip the earliest lot. Could feed a weekly alert. Hold until there's evidence it matters.\n- **Dashboard stock list:** render `expiry_dates` as a chip list / table instead of a comma-separated string. Polish.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Pitfalls & invariants\n\nThese bit us during implementation — flag them in code review.\n\n1. **`syncService.flushOnce()` is single-flight via a `running` flag.** A second concurrent call returns `{sent:0}` immediately and the caller may then refetch against stale server state. **EditStock and Dispense both now `await flushOnce()` before navigation.** Don't reintroduce fire-and-forget.\n\n2. **`refreshStockCache()` must be called after every lot mutation.** The lot helpers (`addLot`, `replaceLots`, `decrementLots`) do this internally — don't bypass them with raw SQL.\n\n3. **New barcodes need `ensureStockRow` before lot writes.** Otherwise `refreshStockCache` finds no parent row to update and the cache stays at 0 while lots exist. Receipts/Issues/Corrections handlers all call it before lot helpers.\n\n4. **The NULL-expiry sentinel hack.** Unique index uses `COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01')` server-side, `COALESCE(expiry_date, '__no_expiry__')` client-side. Don't write `''` as expiry — always `null` or a real ISO date. The route handlers validate this.\n\n5. **Receipt idempotency.** `insertReceipt` is idempotent on `id`. The receipts handler only calls `addLot` when `result.inserted` is true. Same pattern for issues/corrections. Don't add lot writes outside the `inserted` guard or retries will double-count.\n\n6. **The big `QtyStepper` (88px) is for hero inputs only.** Use `CompactStepper` in list rows. We learned this the hard way — using the big one inside `BatchEditor` rows broke the entire UI on the device.\n\n7. **Pre-migration stock dispense needs the field-app fallback.** If you remove the synthetic-lot fallback in `DispenseScreen.tsx`, dispense breaks on every legacy item.\n\n8. **`json_agg` quoting in PG drivers.** Some drivers (pg / pglite) return json columns as strings, others auto-parse. `listStock` and `listLowOrOut` normalise both shapes. Don't assume the array form in db.ts.\n\n---\n\n## 9. How to verify on a real device\n\nThe field-app must be tested on a real Android device (vision-camera, haptics, offline transitions, SQLite migrations don't reproduce reliably in simulator — per `field-app/CLAUDE.md`).\n\n**Golden paths to test:**\n\n- **Receive 10 packs as 3+4+3 groups:**\n  - Open ReceivingScreen, tap \"+ Add another expiry batch\" twice, set qty/expiry on each.\n  - After save, check StockScreen → on_hand = 10.\n  - Open EditStock → see 3 batches preserved.\n  - Check admin dashboard Stock view → `expiry_dates` column shows `\"3@DATE, 4@DATE, 3@DATE\"`.\n\n- **Dispense single-lot product:**\n  - Should show only the big \"HOW MANY?\" stepper (no `LotPicker`).\n  - Confirm decrements correctly.\n\n- **Dispense 3 from a 3-batch product, override FEFO:**\n  - Big stepper to 3 → `LotPicker` appears, FEFO row pre-filled with 3.\n  - Adjust to 1+1+1 across three lots.\n  - Confirm → each lot decrements by 1. Verify in EditStock.\n\n- **EditStock on legacy stock (pre-migration):**\n  - Should seed a single batch from `(on_hand, nearest_expiry)`.\n  - Splitting into batches and saving should write `lots[]` to server.\n\n- **Offline → online sync:**\n  - Receive offline. Verify outbox queues. Bring online → lots appear on dashboard and on other devices.\n\n- **EditStock conflict banner:**\n  - Open EditStock, then dispense from another device in between, then try to save → banner appears, \"Use new count\" collapses to a single batch (known limitation, see Open Items).\n\n---\n\n## 10. Deployment notes\n\n**Engine deploy (Bifrost project `auxilio-v2`, env `dev`, service `ops-engine`):**\n\n```bash\ncd office-ops-engine\ngit push personal main\nbifrost deploy --commit <full-sha>   # ALWAYS pass --commit\n```\n\nWhy `--commit`: per `feedback_bifrost_deploy_commit` memory + `office-ops-engine/CLAUDE.md` — Bifrost tags project-level images by `<project>/<branch>:sha-<short>`, so omitting `--commit` can pick up another service's last image. Past incident: ops-dashboard URL served visitor-web HTML.\n\nSchema migration is in `postgres-schema.ts` and runs automatically at server boot. No manual migration step.\n\n**Dashboard deploy:** no changes required, but if you redeploy: same `--commit` discipline. Build args: `VITE_OPS_API_URL`, `VITE_POSTHOG_KEY`, `VITE_POSTHOG_HOST`.\n\n**Field-app deploy:** JS-only changes — Metro reload picks them up for dev builds. The SQLite migration (v7 → v8) runs at app launch from `runMigrations`. **No native rebuild required.** For an EAS internal build:\n\n```bash\neas build --profile internal --platform android\n```\n\n---\n\n## 11. Key memories (read these too)\n\nThe user's auto-memory at `~/.claude/projects/-Users-appointy-work-OfficeOperationsUmbrella/memory/`:\n\n- `project_inventory_lots.md` — high-level summary of this work + the soft-nudge FEFO stance\n- `feedback_bifrost_deploy_commit.md` — why `--commit` is non-negotiable\n- `project_auxilio_v2.md` — Bifrost project/service/env IDs\n- `project_ameya_stakeholder.md` — cost framing pattern for stakeholder conversations\n- `user_role.md` — user pitches Office Ops designs to leadership; wants disagreement not validation\n\n---\n\n## 12. Quick orientation checklist for the next agent\n\nIf you're picking this up cold, in order:\n\n1. Read `../CLAUDE.md` (umbrella constraints), then `office-ops-engine/CLAUDE.md` and `field-app/CLAUDE.md`.\n2. Read the memories listed in §11.\n3.
